**W2-12D · Real-integration closeout audit (2026-08-17 — this commit, awaiting owner review):** the audit question — *is every backend-capable Provider Portal surface using its real contract in live mode?* — answers YES after two narrow corrections. **The live composition matrix** ([auth-runtime.ts](portal/src/app/auth-runtime.ts) live branch, now structurally test-locked): auth/session/MFA/continuity/logout + `/provider/me` access (A) · org view/profile/storefront state, onboarding view+submit, branches+areas, team/invitations/acceptance (B) · categories/activity types, listings index with authoritative server q/status filtering and the list-card projection, listing detail (C1) · the 13 editor mutations incl. protected-edit revision routing (C2) · the 4 lifecycle actions + provider-safe openRevision status (C3) — **all live; the ONLY unconfigured adapter in the live branch is `bulkImportPort`** (expected deferred; the composition-lock test pins exactly that set). **Two accidental holes found and narrowly fixed:** (1) the live onboarding snapshot still hard-coded `listingCount: null` although the C1 listings read made it computable — it now uses the same bounded authoritative page walk the Dashboard uses (real count for `catalogue.read` holders — contract-proven 2 and 0 on real PostgreSQL; truthfully null for members without the capability, on failure, or on bound exhaustion — never a fabricated zero), restoring the live-mode first-listing onboarding step; (2) the Dashboard catalogue walk could silently return a TRUNCATED catalogue as complete if its defensive 100-page bound exhausted (10,000+ listings) — it now returns honestly unavailable instead (test-locked; a real aggregate read stays the recorded future optimization). **Dashboard truth:** every card derives from live ports (published/needs-attention/awaiting-Himma from the real rows + card projection; branches/org status from the org view; team members from the real staff read; recently-updated from real rows) — no future bookings/revenue metric exists. **Deferred surfaces audited truthful:** bookings/schedule/finance/settings are honest placeholder pages naming their future milestone; W2-10 import preview states "nothing was changed" and its live dry-run is fail-closed unavailable; media stays metadata-only with no fake upload; no fake moderation exists anywhere. **Fixture isolation re-confirmed:** every live module carries a comment-stripped structural sweep; no catch-block returns fixture data; malformed DTOs fail closed (all previously test-locked, re-verified). **Roles:** exactly the seven canonical roles; capabilities from `/provider/me` only; Cognito claims grant nothing (contract-locked). **Cross-org:** profile/branches/team/listings/detail/revisions previously proven; the ONE missing matrix cell — foreign-org LIFECYCLE actions — is now contract-proven not-found-shaped for submit/publish/archive. **Reload/session composition:** the §18 continuity sequence plus the C2/C3 fresh-context journeys already prove refresh → `/provider/me` → real domain reads with no fixture bootstrap. **Cache invalidation:** the audited relationships (profile→shell via accessChanged · branch→onboarding · team→`/provider/me` · listing edit→detail/index/dashboard · price/branch→card · lifecycle→detail/index/filter/dashboard/public) all hold from B/C2/C3 wiring — no stale-data defect found. **Verification (all green this commit):** portal tsc (main + contract) clean · ESLint `--max-warnings=0` clean · **portal Jest 759/759 across 58 suites** (5 new closeout tests: composition lock, walk-exhaustion honesty, live count walk/unknown semantics) · **contract suite 69/69 on real PostgreSQL** (2 new closeout proofs) · production build green/fail-closed · **fixture Playwright 208 passed / 20 by-design skips** · backend untouched · `db:verify` 10/0. **Remaining gaps with explicit future ownership:** real Cognito pool + §14.E′ smokes (operational/infra) · https live-browser harness (real-pool smoke phase) · media binaries/storage (future media milestone) · bulk import engine (future backend/integration milestone) · verification evidence tooling + provider-facing rejection reason + rich revision feedback/history (Internal Admin workstream) · request-new-Activity-Type workflow (admin/support) · aggregate listing count (backend read optimization) · Offer timezone ruling (owner decision, docs/09) · price-option reorder partial-failure (future backend correction) · bookings/schedules/capacity/payments/finance (future platform workstreams, docs/23).

**W2-12C3 · Real listing lifecycle, readiness & revision status integration (2026-08-17 — this commit, awaiting owner review):** the approved W2-9 lifecycle surface now acts through the four REAL named lifecycle routes — same panel, same `ListingLifecyclePort` contract, real backend. **NEW [live-lifecycle-port.ts](portal/src/services/live/live-lifecycle-port.ts)** (live-mode composition in [auth-runtime.ts](portal/src/app/auth-runtime.ts)): `POST .../listings/:programId/submit` (`listings.manage`) · `/publish` · `/pause` · `/archive` (all `listings.publish`), every action CAS (`expectedVersion` pass-through; `staleVersion` terminal — no retry, no version substitution; the panel refetches canonical truth and the provider reconciles). **Exact canonical transitions, server-enforced:** draft|changes_requested → submitted (resubmission IS the same submit action); approved → published (first publication) and paused → published (resume — no separate route); published → paused; published|paused → archived (TERMINAL — no unarchive exists, the response carries no version). Providers can express NO moderation transition (submitted→in_review→approved/changes_requested and every revision decision remain internal operations authority; the live port's 4-operation surface is structurally locked, and the admin decision route refuses provider principals — contract-probed). **Canonical readiness:** submit AND publish completeness is the backend's — an incomplete action answers the structured `programIncomplete` with the exact service `missing[]` vocabulary (an out-of-vocabulary gap fails the mapping closed — nothing fabricated); the editor's local hints stay advisory. **Publication gates:** approval ≠ publication (an approved listing is NOT public until the separate publish act — contract-proven via the real public read); the organization go-live gate maps `organizationNotLive` 1:1 and is never bypassed (a verified-but-not-live org cannot publish, and nothing marks it live as a side effect); `publishedAt` is set once by the backend on first publication and RETAINED across pause/resume (proven byte-equal). **Public-visibility composition (real public routes):** draft/submitted/approved → 404 · publish → 200 (org live + storefront published) · pause → 404 · resume → 200 · archive → 404 terminal. **Authority matrix (server-enforced, contract-proven):** Owner + Org Manager hold submit AND publication lifecycle; Listings Editor submits but cannot publish; Branch Manager submits ONLY within the stricter every-association mutation scope (readable-via-one-branch is NOT submittable while any association lies outside — proven both ways) and cannot publish; coach/front-desk/finance hold nothing. **Provider ProgramRevision status:** the detail's `openRevision {id, state, createdAt, version}` embed IS the provider-safe status read — the REVISION JOURNEY proves the full composition: protected edit on a published listing → real open revision → a completely fresh sign-in sees the truthful pending status with EXACTLY those four fields → the live value stays authoritative and public → no provider decision path exists → internal approval (backend services with a real dual-approved operations-role actor) applies the change and clears the status. Revision existence never leaks (foreign org and out-of-scope BM get the one not-found shape). No reviewer feedback/reason surfaces anywhere (the `request_changes` reasonCode stays provider-unreadable — gap carried; proven the code string never appears in any provider read). **Refresh behavior:** the existing panel invalidation (`['listing', org, id]` + `['listings', org]` prefix) already covers the detail, the index cards/status-filter membership, AND the dashboard summary — the journey proves an archived listing leaves the `published` filter membership. NO backend change (`db:verify` 10/0; no migration). NO visual redesign; no fake moderation timers — live mode shows only actual server state. **Verification (all green this commit):** portal tsc (main + contract) clean · ESLint `--max-warnings=0` clean · **portal Jest 754/754 across 58 suites** (18 new live-lifecycle-port tests: the four routes' wire shape, canonical-state results incl. never-optimistic-in_review, structured incompleteness + out-of-vocabulary fail-closed, go-live gate, stale-terminal, malformed-echo fail-closed, 4-operation POST-only structure, fixture-isolation sweep; + live-composition additions) · **contract suite 67/67 on real PostgreSQL** (NEW [catalogue-lifecycle-contract.test.ts](portal/test-contract/catalogue-lifecycle-contract.test.ts), 14 tests: the §31 submission matrix incl. structured-gap refusal + editor-submits + BM stricter scope both ways + stale · the §32 publication matrix incl. approval-not-public, org-gate, re-checked completeness after branch removal, full role matrix with org-manager positive · §33 pause/resume/archive with publishedAt retention + illegal-source refusals · §34/§36 the revision journey + cross-org/scope sealing · **the §35 END-TO-END journey: authenticate → author → submit → internal approval → not-public → publish → public → pause → hidden → resume → public → archive → terminal, with index card + filter membership following**) · production build green/fail-closed · **fixture Playwright 208 passed / 20 by-design skips** · backend untouched. **Contract gaps (task §46, carried unchanged):** media binary upload/storage · aggregate listing count · request-new-Activity-Type workflow · verification evidence tooling · provider-facing rejection reason · rich revision diff/history · bulk import engine · Offer timezone ruling · price-option reorder partial failure · real Cognito infrastructure/smoke · live-browser e2e harness (https local fake Cognito). **W2-12 catalogue integration now covers reads (C1), authoring mutations (C2), and lifecycle (C3) — NOT marked complete: bulk import and the operational real-Cognito smokes remain, and the next bounded responsibility awaits owner assignment.**

**W2-12C2 · Real listing creation/edit & child-resource mutation integration (2026-08-17 — this commit, awaiting owner review; W2-12C3 NOT started):** the approved W2-8 editor now WRITES through the real Slice-4 catalogue management routes — same screens, same `ListingEditorPort` contract, real backend. **NEW [live-listing-editor-port.ts](portal/src/services/live/live-listing-editor-port.ts)** (live-mode composition in [auth-runtime.ts](portal/src/app/auth-runtime.ts)) implements all 13 approved operations route for route: `POST .../listings` (create — draft-first: the canonical backend id/version returns, state is `draft`, nothing submits/approves/publishes) · `PATCH .../listings/:programId` (CAS dirty-field edit) · branch `POST .../branches` + `POST .../branches/:id/remove` · price options `POST`/`PATCH`/`POST .../archive` · media metadata `POST`/`PATCH`/`POST .../archive` · offers `POST`/`PATCH`/`POST .../end`. **Semantics are server truth, mapped 1:1, never decided in the portal:** the edit-state matrix (draft/changes_requested direct · approved/published/paused review-gated · submitted/in_review/archived locked → `lifecycleConflict`); **protected edits ride the backend's AUTOMATIC ProgramRevision routing** — a sensitive program/price change on a review-gated listing answers `revisionSubmitted` (live values untouched until Himma approves; the detail read exposes exactly the open-revision status — no reviewer feedback, no diff, no decision/withdrawal operation exists anywhere) and a competing sensitive change answers `revisionPending` (at most ONE open revision; contract-proven); **CAS everywhere the wire requires it** — `expectedVersion` passes through untouched, `staleVersion` maps 1:1, the port NEVER retries with a newer version (structural single-call proof) and the existing conflict UX reconciles after a refetch; a foreign/inactive branch is `invalidBranch`) — all server-enforced, contract-proven; Offer windows pass through as the exact wire ISO date-time strings (the timestamptz invariant — NO new timezone policy invented; the unresolved Offer-timezone ruling stays carried); Arabic stays optional and preserved (English-only drafts create cleanly, contract-proven); inactive Activity Types cannot be newly selected (`invalidTaxonomy`, contract-proven); media stays METADATA-only (the editor's Photos section keeps its honest no-upload surface; binaries/storage remain the carried gap); a malformed mutation echo FAILS CLOSED to unavailable — canonical success only ever comes from the backend response, and the module is structurally isolated from fixture code (sweep + no-public-channel + no-access-change-claim tests). **Query invalidation stayed the existing targeted page-level pattern** — pricing and media sections now ALSO invalidate the listings index (their summaries ride the C1 list-card row); no global cache clears. **Price-option reorder:** no dedicated reorder route exists and none was invented — ordering remains per-option `sortHint` PATCHes through the same CAS path; the recorded partial-failure gap stays carried unchanged. **LIFECYCLE stays fail-closed unconfigured in live mode** (submit/resubmit/publish/resume/pause/archive → W2-12C3; **W2-12A final correction · Concrete Cognito ProviderTokenRefresher (2026-08-17 — OWNER-APPROVED; W2-12A CLOSED at `b030b0a` — this commit, awaiting owner review; on approval W2-12A is FULLY CLOSED):** the last code-completeness gap is closed: the production backend now has a REAL Cognito implementation of the refresh boundary, executable the moment a pool exists. **[CognitoRefreshHttpClient](backend/src/modules/identity/providers/cognito/cognito-token-refresher.ts)** performs the exact **`InitiateAuth` / `AuthFlow: REFRESH_TOKEN_AUTH`** operation against the pool's regional endpoint (derived from `COGNITO_ISSUER`) for the designated PUBLIC portal app client — the same unsigned Cognito IdP JSON API the approved D1 architecture already uses browser-side, so no AWS credential, no client secret (a confidential client would contradict the browser-direct D1 flow — documented), and no SDK dependency (matching the repo's concrete-adapter style: jose for JWKS, platform HTTP for provider calls, injectable seams for tests); `createCognitoTokenRefresher(config)` wraps it in the existing normalization. **Token mapping is exact:** refreshed access token; ID token only when returned; a NEW refresh token passed through ONLY when Cognito actually rotates one — never fabricated (the server cookie keeps the original otherwise). **Error mapping:** NotAuthorized/UserNotFound/PasswordResetRequired → `invalidRefreshToken` (session-invalid; the route clears the channel); network/5xx/throttling/unknown → `providerUnavailable` (outage NEVER converts to revocation); raw AWS payloads/request-ids/token material never escape (name-only errors with a fixed safe message — test-proven). **Production adapter-set coherence:** the fake-only logout gap is closed too — **[CognitoSessionRevoker](backend/src/modules/identity/providers/cognito/cognito-session-revoker.ts)** implements `ProviderSessionRevoker` via `RevokeToken` (public client) for session-scope revocation with cookie material, and is HONEST about public-client limits: `allSessions`/no-token → typed `notSupported` (Cognito global sign-out is an admin-credential operation the architecture does not hold; Himma `login_session` denial is the authority regardless, and undelivered revocations stay retryable per the port contract). **Configuration:** `COGNITO_ISSUER` + `COGNITO_CLIENT_IDS` as before, plus `COGNITO_REFRESH_CLIENT_ID` (must be an accepted client id; defaults to the sole entry, REQUIRED when several exist — ambiguity refuses at startup). **Production composition ([session-continuity-composition.ts](backend/src/modules/identity/providers/cognito/session-continuity-composition.ts)):** `createCognitoSessionContinuity(nodeEnv, env)` is the one factory a live startup uses — constructs ONLY concrete providers (no fake importable from it), returns undefined with no Cognito config (channel absent, 404), and THROWS at startup on ambiguous refresh client, insecure production cookies, or a production channel with no declared portal origin. **Verification (all green this commit):** backend tsc + ESLint clean · **backend Jest 714/714 across 57 suites** (15 new adapter/composition tests: exact REFRESH_TOKEN_AUTH wire shape + designated client id + refresh-token-only-to-Cognito · optional-token mapping incl. no-fabricated-rotation + rotation passthrough · session-invalid vs outage distinction incl. throttling · no-AWS-leak proof · fake/concrete port-contract parity · config matrix incl. ambiguous-client refusal · production composition selects concrete providers + every fail-closed gap · concrete revoker RevokeToken + honest notSupported/outage · **§10 route compatibility: the REAL `/auth/refresh` on real PostgreSQL over the CONCRETE adapter — verification → liveness/CAS → CSRF rotation → usable bearer**) · `db:verify` green (10 migrations, 0 pending — no DB change) · portal contract suite 15/15 unchanged (its harness keeps the deterministic fake, proving fake/live injection separation). **Operationally pending, never claimed: non-prod pool provisioning · live REFRESH_TOKEN_AUTH + SOFTWARE_TOKEN_MFA smokes · live-browser cookie inspection · production region/DR/email/Apple-Google identifiers.** W2-12B remains NOT started.

**W2-12A correction · Production browser session continuity (docs/26 §4.7(9)/§14.E — 2026-08-17, this commit, awaiting owner review):** the one conditional-approval blocker is closed: a hard reload no longer signs the provider out. **Backend (the §14.E channel is auth infrastructure — implemented exactly per canon, NO domain/schema change, NO migration):** new `ProviderTokenRefresher` port ([providers/refresh.ts](backend/src/modules/identity/providers/refresh.ts)) with the Cognito implementation behind an injectable client (the concrete SDK client stays §14.E′ real-pool work) and a deterministic fake; new [auth-session-cookies.ts](backend/src/modules/identity/http/auth-session-cookies.ts) (config parsing + strict serialization + timing-safe double-submit + origin allowlist); **`POST /auth/session`** now accepts the refresh token ONCE and moves it into **`himma_refresh` — `Path=/auth; SameSite=Strict; Max-Age=<AUTH_REFRESH_COOKIE_MAX_AGE_SECONDS, default 30d>; HttpOnly; Secure`** plus the readable **`himma_csrf`** double-submit cookie (same attributes minus HttpOnly), returning the CSRF value in JSON (the refresh token appears in NO response body — test-locked); new **`GET /auth/csrf`** (reload bootstrap entry: reissues the double-submit value to an allowlisted origin holding the refresh cookie); new **`POST /auth/refresh`** — origin allowlist → timing-safe CSRF check → cookie → provider refresh via the port → access-token verification → **the §9.4 Himma transaction (`checkSessionLiveness` + CAS last-seen): a still-valid Cognito refresh token can NEVER bypass `login_session` revocation/expiry** — refusal clears the cookie channel (no loop fuel), rotation rotates the cookie; **`POST /auth/logout`** clears both cookies, uses the cookie refresh token for provider revocation, and revokes the session-of-record (a retained stale jar cannot re-authenticate — test-proven). New `csrfRejected` (403) outcome; minimal config-driven CORS/preflight for `PORTAL_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (credentialed; nothing hardcoded; production REFUSES `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=false` and http origins — parse-time errors). Registration is gated on the new optional `sessionContinuity` deps — absent ⇒ byte-identical pre-correction behavior. **Portal:** the live runtime presents the refresh token EXACTLY ONCE at establishment and **drops it from JS memory when the channel engages** (csrfToken in the response); `bootstrap()` = `GET /auth/csrf` → `POST /auth/refresh` (ONE attempt, no loops — any refusal lands the existing W2-2 sign-in UX; deep links survive because the URL survives the reload and the guard chain re-resolves access); the double-submit value lives in adapter memory only; the central API client gained `withCredentials`/`csrfToken` (auth-boundary calls only — domain calls stay bearer-only); logout rides the cookie channel (the legacy in-memory pass-through remains only for a backend without the channel). Access tokens stay memory-only; NO browser storage anywhere (structural sweeps + spies unchanged and passing). **Verification (all green this commit):** backend tsc + ESLint clean · **backend Jest 699/699 across 56 suites** (17 new session-continuity tests: exact cookie attributes · no-echo · CSRF missing/incorrect/cross-origin refused · valid same-origin refresh → usable bearer · revoked/expired `login_session` refuse + channel cleared · provider-invalidation vs outage distinction · rotation · refresh-cannot-mint-authority · membership-stays-PostgreSQL-authoritative · logout architecture · production insecure-config refusal) · `db:verify` green (10 migrations, 0 pending — schema untouched) · portal tsc (main + contract) + ESLint clean · **portal Jest 643/643 across 55 suites** (bootstrap/reload, single-attempt no-loop, one-time refresh handover, cookie-channel logout, legacy fallback) · **contract suite 15/15 on real PostgreSQL incl. the §18 sequence: sign-in/MFA → continuity established → simulated browser memory loss → cookie/CSRF refresh → usable access → real `/provider/me` memberships → forced `login_session` revocation → refresh REFUSED and channel cleared** (+ membership-revocation-across-reload, logout-then-reload, foreign-org-after-reload) · production build green/fail-closed · **fixture Playwright regression green** (no visual change; fixture mode untouched). **Still operationally pending (never claimed): real Cognito pool + the §14.E′ SOFTWARE_TOKEN_MFA and REFRESH_TOKEN_AUTH smokes (which also ratify the app-client flow configuration) · production region/DR/email/Apple-Google identifiers. HttpOnly enforcement is a browser guarantee — proven here through exact Set-Cookie attributes; a live-browser cookie inspection belongs to the real-pool smoke.** W2-12B remains NOT started.

**W2-12A · Real provider authentication, session & access integration (2026-08-17 — this commit, awaiting owner review; W2-12B NOT started):** the portal's W2-2 access/session seam gained its REAL production implementation while the fixture path stays fully intact. **Mode model (explicit, fail-closed):** `VITE_PORTAL_AUTH_MODE` resolves `fixture` (Jest/Playwright/local demo/walkthroughs — unchanged) · `live` (explicit in EVERY environment) · anything else/absent → production `unconfigured` (safe unavailable surface). Live mode with ANY missing value (`VITE_API_BASE_URL`, `VITE_COGNITO_ISSUER`, `VITE_COGNITO_CLIENT_ID` — public browser-safe identifiers, configuration not secrets) fails CLOSED to the unconfigured runtime; production NEVER silently falls back to fixtures and no fixture identity/control is installed in a live composition (test-proven). **Live architecture (docs/26 D1/Amendment A1.1 exactly):** the browser authenticates with Cognito directly through the ONE portal Cognito boundary ([cognito-api.ts](portal/src/auth/live/cognito-api.ts): `InitiateAuth` USER_PASSWORD_AUTH → optional `SOFTWARE_TOKEN_MFA` challenge → `RespondToAuthChallenge`; `RevokeToken` best-effort; provider errors normalized to the semantic vocabulary — the documented respond-context `NotAuthorizedException → challengeExpired` mapping included); the issued token pair is presented ONCE to the real **`POST /auth/session`** (first-login/identity resolution + Himma `login_session` establishment); the Cognito ACCESS token is the only API bearer; Himma PostgreSQL liveness (issuer+subject+`origin_jti`) stays the revocation authority; **provider access comes ONLY from the real `GET /provider/me`** ({organizationId, displayName, role, branchScope, organizationState} mapped field-for-field, rows validated — a contract-violating row fails the WHOLE resolution closed; Cognito claims/groups grant NOTHING). Step-up rides the REAL routes (`POST /auth/step-up/totp/begin`→`/complete`, `POST /auth/step-up/recovery-code`) behind the unchanged one-call W2-2 seam; logout = `POST /auth/logout` with the refresh token passed through the route's documented EPHEMERAL provider-revocation channel (direct `RevokeToken` fallback if Himma is unreachable), always landing signed-out locally. **Token ownership (task §6):** ALL token material lives in the live-runtime closure in memory only — never localStorage/sessionStorage/IndexedDB (structural sweeps + runtime spies), never React state, never URLs/query strings (proven across every recorded call), never logs; the Authorization header is constructed in exactly ONE place (the central [api/client.ts](portal/src/api/client.ts) transport, structurally locked). **The docs/26 §4.7(9) refresh-token cookie channel needed §14.E backend cookie/CSRF wiring that did not yet exist — a hard reload started signed out. This was the conditional-approval blocker, closed by the session-continuity correction above.** **Session truth:** a 401 `sessionExpired`/`invalidAccessToken` on any authorized call drops tokens and pushes the ONE canonical interrupt; SessionProvider now ALSO clears the TanStack Query cache on sign-out and on session expiry (both modes — no stale provider data survives its session, test-proven), and the existing guard chain (signed-out → sign-in with safe return-to · MFA gates · noMembership · workspace-unavailable) is unchanged. UX untouched (task §20): no screen redesigned. **Contract tests (task §18 — REAL vs SIMULATED documented):** a new node-environment suite ([portal/test-contract/](portal/test-contract/), `npm run test:contract`, own tsconfig) runs the LIVE adapter + access port against the REAL `buildApp` listening on real freshly-migrated PostgreSQL — real routes, policies, `login_session` rows, staff memberships, outcome codes; ONLY the external Cognito HTTP endpoint is simulated (a deterministic fake minting tokens through the backend's own FakeAccessTokenVerifier/FakeAuthProviderAdapter — no backend file modified). 11 contract tests: full MFA sign-in → session-of-record row → exact multi-org membership DTOs (incl. branch-scoped list) · wrong-TOTP/consumed-challenge semantics · single-factor path · one safe invalid-credentials class · authority-suggesting scopes + zero memberships ⇒ `memberships: []` · foreign/ghost org ids byte-identical `notFound` (no leak) · forced `login_session` revocation ⇒ tokens dropped + canonical interrupt · membership revocation ⇒ empty memberships on next authoritative resolution (live session intact) · logout revokes the real session row and the bearer dies · real two-legged step-up completes/refuses. **Verification (all green this commit):** portal tsc clean (+ the contract tsconfig) · ESLint `--max-warnings=0` clean · **Jest 641/641 across 55 suites** (23 live-adapter mapping/hygiene tests incl. storage spies + source sweeps + URL-leak proofs; 9 mode/fail-closed composition tests; 2 session-cache-clearing tests; the two W2-2-era structural sweeps DELIBERATELY narrowed for the new boundary: bearer construction allowed in exactly api/client.ts, Cognito vocabulary allowed only in the auth boundary — every other module stays claims-free; all W2 suites preserved) · **contract suite 11/11 on real PostgreSQL** · production build green/fail-closed · **Playwright fixture regression 208 passed / 20 by-design skips** at 1440/1024/390. **Operationally NOT certified (never claimed, task §17):** no real Cognito pool exists in any environment — no live smoke ran; outstanding: non-prod pool provisioning + the §14.E′ real-pool SOFTWARE_TOKEN_MFA smoke (which also ratifies the USER_PASSWORD_AUTH app-client flow choice) · §14.E backend refresh-cookie/CSRF wiring (reload-surviving portal sessions) · production region/DR (docs/23 §18.3) · production email · Apple/Google identifiers. **W2-12B (NEXT, do not start without owner approval): domain read/mutation integration over this authenticated transport** — profile/onboarding · branches/areas · team · listings read + editor + lifecycle · taxonomy (`ActivityTypeReadPort`, the `CategoryReadPort` recorded at `52d051c`) · `ListingCardPort` list-card projection (recorded Class-B backend requirement) · import dry-run; carried gaps unchanged (media binaries · provider moderation feedback · revision diff · import engine · offer timezone · reorder partial-failure UX · request-new-Activity-Type workflow).

**W2 owner interaction correction — listing creation & editor input model (owner-approved and CLOSED at `52d051c`):** the owner found the listing workflow leaned on dropdowns without distinguishing WHO owns each field ("If everything is a dropdown, how does the provider add their own information?"). Every listing field is now explicitly classified and rendered by its interaction category: **(A) provider-AUTHORED content** (title, descriptions, eligibility notes, option/offer labels) = direct text inputs — nothing implies Himma predefines provider content; **(B) Himma-MANAGED controlled vocabulary** (Activity Type; standardized eligibility vocabulary) = selection from the active canon, never free-text creation — the Activity Type select became a production [searchable combobox](portal/src/pages/listings/editor/activity-type-combobox.tsx) (ARIA combobox semantics, arrow/Enter/Escape keyboard support, label search over ACTIVE taxonomy only, category context via a NEW `CategoryReadPort` mirroring the real public `GET /catalogue/categories` read, clear-selection control, polite result-count announcements) whose no-match state is the TRUTHFUL fallback ("Can't find the activity you need? … ask the Himma team through Support" → the existing Support route) — **no "create new activity type" operation exists anywhere and typing can never submit taxonomy (test-locked)**; **(C) provider-OWNED business entities** (Branches, ProgramPriceOptions, Offers, media) = create/manage workflows over the provider's own objects: Locations rows now carry name + area context and gained the `+ Add a new branch` navigation to the REAL W2-5 `/branches/new` route, rendered ONLY for `branch.create` holders (Owner/Org Manager — test-proven absent for Listings Editor and Branch Manager); Pricing keeps the W2-8 `+ Add price option` creation model with the kind select replaced by a **"Price option type" chip choice** (Drop-in/Monthly/Term/Camp/Package/Free — exact canon, D-S4-1 untouched, integer-fils money unchanged); Offers keep `+ Add offer` with the four-kind canon as chips (an edited offer states its kind is fixed instead of rendering a disabled select); **(D) finite enums** = segmented/chip controls, not dropdowns (Setting = Indoor|Outdoor chips; audience = the five-value chip set "Who is this activity for?"; Skill level stays an honest select). **Create Listing restructured** (drafts stay deliberately lightweight): "Basic information" (title\*, activity type\*, setting, description) → collapsed **"+ Add Arabic content (optional)" disclosure** (English-only launch: Arabic optional, auto-opens when content exists, collapsing NEVER loses entered values — test-proven; nothing about Arabic architecture removed) → "Who can join" (audience chips, all-ages/age bounds, skill, notes) → "Create draft listing" with the draft-first truth ("Start with the basics… complete locations, pricing, photos and offers after creating the draft"); requiredness is one clear convention ("Fields marked \* are required") instead of scattered "(optional)" labels; forms cap at a readable 640px on desktop (no database-admin full-bleed fields). **NO backend/schema/API change; no authority/lifecycle/CAS/revision behavior changed** (W2-8/W2-9 matrices test-locked: protected-field routing, revisionPending locks, frozen states, BM every-rule scope all unchanged). The `com.himma.dev` application/bundle identifier is committed in `app.json` since `b5fbe49` as a **development-only placeholder** — the production identifiers remain an open owner decision (docs/23 §18).

**First Android smoke pass (risk discovery, NOT full validation — no screen upgraded):** Home, Discover, Search+suggestions, Results, Program Details, booking participant step (skip rule fired for the dateless package), summary, and checkout all rendered and operated; hardware back popped exactly one step everywhere with state intact; checkout inert-submit contract held natively (unready CTA inert with named blocker; ready CTA inert; method selection persisted); carousels scrolled correctly inside vertical scroll; zero FATAL/JS errors in logcat on both AVDs.

**Findings logged (not fixed — separate task):**
1. **Native defect — camp price unit lost:** cards render `AED 850 /` / `AED 990 /` on Android where web renders `AED 850/week` / `AED 990/week` (verified same-build web comparison). Font-metric/wrap divergence class (docs/12 §4).
2. **QA-affordance gap:** `?qa-revalidate` (and `?qa-scenario`) are guarded `window.location` reads — native deep links (`himma://booking/...?qa-revalidate=...`, warm and cold) cannot trigger the CheckoutIssueCard review states on device until the params move to router params.
3. **Environment notes:** emulator Gboard sits in floating-toolbar mode, so docked-keyboard avoidance is still unexercised (low risk — transactional flows have no text inputs); the small AVD needed a wifi toggle (`svc wifi disable/enable`) after first cold boot before Metro was reachable.

**iOS: environment complete; launch verified; interactive smoke pass pending one owner command.** Xcode 26.6 (17F113) installed and selected (`/Applications/Xcode.app/Contents/Developer`), first-launch components done, iOS 26.5 simulator runtime (23F77) installed via `xcodebuild -downloadPlatform iOS`. CocoaPods installed via Homebrew (Expo auto-installed it; `pod install` needs `LANG=en_US.UTF-8`). `npx expo run:ios` **Build Succeeded**; Himma launched and Home render-verified on **both** iPhone 17 and Himma-SE (evidence in `artifacts/native-smoke-ios/`). Notably iOS renders `Your week` and `/week` correctly — narrowing finding 1 above to an **Android-only loss of the trailing word "week"** (headings and price units). **Interactive iOS smoke pass (2026-08-05, after the owner's `xcode-select` fix): DONE on iPhone 17** — deep-link `Open in "Himma"?` acceptance routed correctly into the booking flow (skip rule fired on the cold link); participant step contract (disabled Continue inert with `Choose who is attending`, explained `Not suitable` rows, selection → `Booking for you`); summary intact; checkout unready CTA inert with named blocker; `Card payment` selection → ready CTA with price label; **ready CTA inert (no-submit contract holds on iOS)**; edge-swipe-back popped checkout → intact summary → participant → Home; Search live suggestions working with typed input. Evidence: `artifacts/native-smoke-ios/` (8 captures). ## Native correction task (2026-08-05, post-P0-smoke — owner-approved as `89deda0`–`b96d50e`, fast-forwarded into `main` 2026-08-06)

**A — status-bar collision (Program Details + Provider Storefront): FIXED on all platforms.** Root cause: both detail surfaces scroll content under the transparent status bar with no top mask. Fix: shared **[StatusBarScrim](src/components/domain/status-bar-scrim.tsx)** — a safe-area-height `Animated.View` (`pointerEvents="none"`, native-driver opacity) that fades in over the 56-pt band before the hero/cover bottom reaches `insets.top`; renders nothing when the top inset is 0 (web preview unchanged). Wired into [program-details-screen.tsx](src/features/details/program-details-screen.tsx) (`HERO_HEIGHT` 264) and [provider-storefront-screen.tsx](src/features/details/provider-storefront-screen.tsx) (`COVER_HEIGHT` 220 — owner-directed extension after the collision reproduced there; identical structure). Verified: no collision when scrolled and edge-to-edge hero intact at rest on Pixel 7, 360-dp Android, iPhone 17, and Himma-SE, both surfaces; long + short content; no jump/flicker/duplicate header; back/swipe navigation unaffected.

**B — Android trailing-word loss ("Your" / "AED 850 /"): FIXED, single root cause.** RN-Android (Fabric) lays a `Text` out at its measured width; fractional widths (Manrope advances, negative letterSpacing) get floored by Android's integer text layout, silently wrapping the last word onto a clipped second line. String-dependent — "Your week" broke while "My week" didn't. Three structural fixes, no per-string or per-platform copy: [section-header.tsx](src/components/ui/section-header.tsx) title `flexGrow: 1` (layout width > measured width; letterSpacing kept); [program-card.tsx](src/components/domain/program-card.tsx) amount+unit merged into one nested `Text` (single paragraph, true text baselines); [compact-program-row.tsx](src/components/domain/compact-program-row.tsx) unit `paddingRight: 1` (floor loss < 1pt; preserves the documented whole-unit wrap at 360). Verified: 412-dp + 360-dp Android (incl. font scale 1.3), iPhone 17, Himma-SE, web QA suites. **Watch for:** the same latent defect class exists for any tightly-measured standalone `Text` — if a trailing word vanishes on Android again, reach for these three patterns.

**C — QA params are now Expo Router params (dev-gated): DONE.** `?qa-scenario` ([account-context.tsx](src/state/account-context.tsx)) and `?qa-revalidate` ([booking-session-context.tsx](src/state/booking-session-context.tsx) + [booking/_layout.tsx](src/app/booking/[programId]/_layout.tsx)) no longer read `window.location`; they read `useGlobalSearchParams` (query params ride the *focused* route — a layout's `useLocalSearchParams` never sees them on native) behind `__DEV__` gates, so production builds compile the affordance out. Capture is a **first-valid-wins latch** (conditional setState during render): native navigation state hydrates a render *after* mount, so mount-time capture misses cold deep links; once latched, the value is fixed for the provider's lifetime — the same boundaries as before (account: session; revalidate: booking-flow lifetime via the `key={programId}` remount). All seven revalidation codes now render on native — verified priceChanged/sessionFull/participantIneligible on Android and iPhone 17 via `himma://booking/<id>?qa-revalidate=<code>` cold links. `qa-fail`/`qa-nocount` were already router-param reads. Pure validators `scenarioFromParam`/`qaRevalidateFromParam` replace the search-string parsers (unit tests updated, 408 total).

**Correction-task verification (final, incl. the storefront/Android scrim extension):** tsc clean · ESLint `--max-warnings=0` clean · Jest 408/408 (23 suites) · expo-doctor 20/20 · all nine Playwright QA suites 808/808 (checkout 210 exercising every revalidation state through the router-param path on web; Playwright baselines with sub-pixel/decode-noise-only diffs were reverted per the screenshot policy) · native builds and launches re-verified on demo_phone, Himma_Small_360, iPhone 17, Himma-SE · zero fatal native or JS errors on any target. Curated evidence: `artifacts/native-fix-review/` (per-fix, per-device), `artifacts/native-smoke-{android,ios}/` (representative smoke subset). No screen is upgraded to "native validated" — that requires the full docs/12 §10 checklist (Dynamic Type, VoiceOver/TalkBack, reduce motion, per-screen sweep), which is the next scheduled native-validation task.

## First full native-validation audit (2026-08-06, on `main` at `b96d50e` — full-certification verification tier)

**Scope:** every implemented customer surface (Home, Discover, Search + suggestions, Results, Map, Program Details, Provider Storefront, booking selection/participant/summary, checkout, all seven revalidation states, error/empty/unavailable/cold-link recovery) × four targets (iPhone 17, Himma-SE 375×667, Pixel 7 412 dp, 360-dp Android) × the docs/12 §10 rows (normal type, ~135 % type, screen-reader semantics, Reduced Motion, dark-system-setting confirmation, safe areas/system bars, scroll + nested horizontal gestures, swipe-back/hardware back, focus order/labels/radio/disabled state, touch targets, image + scroll performance, backgrounding, cold + warm deep links, software keyboard, long-text/narrow-width). Validation-only pass — no fixes were implemented. Curated evidence: `artifacts/native-validation-audit/` (38 files; per-target folders; seven-code revalidation sheets per target).

**Method notes (environment-honest):** iOS Dynamic Type via `simctl ui content_size extra-extra-extra-large` (~1.35); Android via `font_scale 1.35`. Reduced Motion via the Accessibility defaults key (iOS — the Settings toggle itself resists synthetic taps) and zeroed animation scales (Android), functionally exercised. iOS screen-reader semantics read from the accessibility tree via `idb ui describe-all`; Android via `uiautomator` (checkable/checked/enabled/content-desc) — VoiceOver cannot run in the Simulator, so live screen-reader runtime remains a physical-device item. Software keyboard: hardware-keyboard disconnect + device reboot (both iOS simulators), `show_ime_with_hard_keyboard` (Android; docked Gboard exercised on the 360 target — this closes the previously standing docked-IME gap for Search; demo_phone still runs the floating toolbar).

**Result: zero blockers, zero high defects.** Everything explicitly re-verified held on all four targets: StatusBarScrim on Details **and** Storefront (opaque under the status bar when scrolled, invisible at rest, no flicker) · complete `Your week` / `/week` / `per session` text (Android trailing-word fix B holding everywhere, incl. price-comparison rows at 1.35 on 360 dp) · all seven CheckoutIssueCard states per target with correct state label, headline, structured old → new comparison, `No payment has been made.` reassurance, single recovery CTA, no payment controls · issue cards under large type on every target (tightest case 360 dp × 1.35 passes; SE × xxxL passes) · sticky-footer clearance and full multi-line blocker wrap (never truncated) · **no payment submission** (ready CTA inert on paid and free checkouts on every target; zero route changes, zero dialogs) · no legal-acceptance or booking-confirmation claim anywhere · QA params remain `__DEV__`-gated router params (verified in source at `b96d50e`) · no accessibility label or live region claims payment/reservation/booking success (checked on the exposed trees). Spoken semantics verified: payment radio `checked/unchecked`, unready CTA `enabled:false` + named blocker, ineligible rows disabled-with-reason, issue-card reading order (state label → headline → support → paired price-comparison label → reassurance → recovery). Backgrounding/reopen preserved state everywhere tested; cold + warm `himma://` links routed correctly (cold checkout links redirect to the flow start; unknown ids render recovery; cold-link back control falls back per `booking-navigation`/detail rules). Dark system setting leaves the app intentionally light with readable status bars on all four targets. Reduced Motion left every exercised surface fully functional. Zero native FATALs, zero JS errors, zero Metro errors across the entire audit (checked per platform).

**Defects (owner review; recommended correction order):** — *A1–A3 corrected 2026-08-06 in the approved correction package (see "A1–A3 correction package" below); A4 stands as recorded environment debt.*
1. **A1 · medium** — Android hardware back at a **cold-deep-linked booking-flow start** finishes the activity (exits to launcher) while the on-screen back chip navigates to Program Details (`booking-selection-screen.tsx:85`/`booking-participant-screen.tsx` fallback). Divergence between the two back affordances mid-flow; normal in-app chains pop exactly one step with state preserved (verified). Recommend intercepting hardware back on the flow-start screen to mirror the chip fallback. (Back-at-Home-root exiting the app is standard Android and not part of this defect.)
2. **A2 · medium (accessibility)** — Home's inert `Your week` card exposes "Your week. Opens your full schedule" to screen readers while the card is inert until HMA-024 (docs/09 §17.2 contract covers visual press feedback, but the spoken label promises navigation that does not happen). Recommend dropping the promise from the accessible label until Bookings ships. Same-class check recommended for other inert items with aspirational hints.
3. **A3 · low (Dynamic Type cosmetic)** — Results scope-tab segment labels break mid-word at ≥1.35 ("Categorie/s" on iOS xxxL, "Program/s · Provider/s · Categori/es" on 360 dp × 1.35). Readable but ugly; size/wrap the segment for large type.
4. **A4 · low (dev-environment only)** — LogBox warning toasts during the audit: Android occurrences are "Cannot connect to Expo CLI" (dev client's DevTools socket check uses `10.0.2.2:8081` while the bundle rides `adb reverse`; environment artifact). One iPhone 17 toast right after enabling Reduce Motion (text not captured — no debugger attached; consistent with Reanimated's dev-only reduced-motion advisory). No production impact; confirm/silence during the correction task.

**Observations (not defects):** floating overlay chips overlap scrolled content headings on Details/Storefront — approved floating-chip pattern, revisit at rebrand if desired · sticky-footer price label ellipsizes at large type/narrow width — documented behavior (full label in the price block and the accessible CTA label; the readiness blocker wraps in full instead) · Map opened during an active search session shows query-scoped counts including zero-count areas — correct session behavior · dev cold starts run ~10–14 s over Metro (dev-only) · `Upcoming activity` card is deliberately inert until HMA-024 (source-documented) — its chevron affordance is part of approved design.

**Performance:** smooth vertical scrolling and carousel-in-scroll on all four targets; no gesture fights; images decode without visible pop-in after first paint; no stutter at simulator/emulator level. (Real-device image-decode performance remains a physical-device item.)

**Keyboard findings:** Search is the only input surface; the input is top-anchored so the keyboard can never cover it; suggestions update live above the keyboard on all four targets; `returnKeyType` search key present; typing works through both docked and floating IMEs.

**Verification tier used:** full certification (branch integration + first full device matrix — per the 2026-08-06 tiered-verification policy). tsc/ESLint/Jest/Playwright were not re-run this session: no `src/` code changed (validation-only), and the identical tree at `b96d50e` carries the Commit-19-era full green run (tsc clean · ESLint clean · Jest 408/408 · expo-doctor 20/20 · Playwright 808/808) recorded above.

## A1–A3 correction package (2026-08-06, after audit approval at `62a3f04`)

Three independent commits, one per concern; A4 untouched (recorded dev-environment debt — no production build has reproduced it).

1. **A1 — `fix(booking): align Android hardware-back fallback`.** New `createFlowStartBackHandler` policy + `useFlowStartHardwareBack` hook in `booking-navigation.ts` (unit-tested), registered focus-scoped and Android-only on the two flow-start screens. Handler declines the event whenever `router.canGoBack()` — the navigator still pops exactly one screen, so normal chains and the draft are untouched — and only the no-history flow-start case mirrors the chip's `replace(programHref)` fallback. Nothing outside the booking flow is intercepted; `replace` cannot create a back loop.
2. **A2 — `fix(a11y): remove promises from inert controls`.** `Your week` card label reduced to `Your week` (promise restored only when Bookings ships). Full same-class sweep of every implemented surface found no other inert control whose label, hint, role, or copy promises unavailable navigation/functionality — the remaining inert items carry the docs/09 §17.2/§21/§22 descriptive-only contracts, and components with optional `onPress` already gate their hints on it. Single confirmed instance.
3. **A3 — `fix(results): preserve tab labels at larger text`.** Scope-tab labels render whole on one line and shrink toward (never below) base size (`adjustsFontSizeToFit`, floor 0.75 of the scaled size — docs/12 §4 dense-control allowance) with 4 dp segment padding for separation. Normal-scale rendering unchanged; no Results redesign. (The package's recorded observation — `CompactProgramRow` titles breaking long words at 1.35 × 360 dp — was corrected separately on owner instruction; see the card-title correction below.)

**Card-title long-word correction (2026-08-06, owner-approved follow-up · `7d3b870 fix(results): wrap long card-title words whole at large text`):** at ~135 % scale on narrow widths a `CompactProgramRow` title whose longest word exceeded the ~148 dp title column split mid-word without a hyphen on Android (`Beginner C/alisthenics`) and tail-truncated inside the word on iOS (`Fundament…`). Root-caused empirically: Android's break strategy renders word overflow as a bare split (greedy instead tail-ellipsizes mid-word), auto-fit APIs accept Android's mid-word-wrapped layout as "fitting", and **`maxFontSizeMultiplier` is inert on this RN version's Android build** (prop applied at mount or updated — layout identical; worth knowing for future capping work; the dock's 1.3 cap and CTA 1.4 caps should be re-checked on a physical-device pass). Fix in `src/utils/text-fit.ts` + the row component: a unit-tested detector reads real `onTextLayout` lines and reports any word cut; on detection the title steps its rendered size toward — never below — base size via style (remounting per step, since Android fires `onTextLayout` only on mount), and iOS additionally uses `adjustsFontSizeToFit` (its fit definition is exactly the iOS truncation mode; iOS layout events report un-ellipsized lines, so the detector alone cannot see iOS truncation), floored at base size. At normal font scale the factor clamps to 1 — approved rendering structurally unchanged. Verified per the correction's tiered policy: whole-word titles at ~1.35 and unchanged normal-type rendering on `Himma_Small_360` and `Himma-SE` (screenshots in session evidence) · focused catalogue/results/utils Jest 73/73 (11 new text-fit tests) · full tsc clean · targeted ESLint clean · Results QA suite green, zero console errors · zero FATAL/JS errors on both targets. Full matrix not re-run (no shared regression surfaced). Catalogue data, card layout, and product behavior untouched.

**Verification (tiered, per the correction-task policy):** full tsc clean · full ESLint clean · full Jest 410/410 (2 new A1 policy tests) · Home, Results, booking, and checkout QA suites all green with zero console errors (Results re-run after the final styling tweak) · **A1 on both AVDs** (demo_phone + Himma_Small_360): dated and skip-rule cold links land on the flow start and hardware back replaces to the correct Program Details with the app foregrounded; second back exits from Details (standard root behavior, no loop); warm chain participant→selection→Details pops one step per press with the selected session preserved · **A2/A3 at normal and ~135 %** (`font_scale 1.35` / `content_size extra-extra-extra-large`) on 360-dp Android, iPhone 17, and Himma-SE: accessibility trees (uiautomator / `idb ui describe-all`) expose `Your week` with no promise; scope tabs show whole, separated labels with no mid-word breaks · **iOS swipe-back unregressed** (iPhone 17: participant→selection→Details edge swipes pop one step each, draft intact) · zero Android FATALs, zero ReactNativeJS errors, zero iOS app-process errors on all four targets. The full four-target certification matrix was not re-run: focused verification surfaced no shared regression (per the correction-task verification policy).

**Environment notes for the next session:** Android font-scale changes recreate the activity and re-deliver the last `VIEW` intent (re-navigate before capturing evidence); the SE run briefly tripped the RN element inspector via a synthetic tap — relaunching the app clears it.

## Canonical domain specification (2026-08-06 — docs/24, conditionally approved; Amendment A1 applied, awaiting final owner approval)

The docs/23 §16 P0 deliverable "§5–§7 canonical-model specification" is drafted as `docs/24_CANONICAL_DOMAIN_SPECIFICATION.md` — a documentation-and-architecture-only commit (no backend code, schema, or `src/` change). It expands docs/23 §5–§7 into the implementation-ready model all seven workstreams share: accounts/identities (identity spine, one-`self`-participant invariant, staff invitations, admin roles, break-glass) · catalogue/supply (taxonomy, programs/listings, schedules, sessions, camp weeks, the uniform capacity-unit interface, owner-final eligibility, quotes, policy-template refs) · booking/inventory (client-only drafts, server quotes, atomic capacity holds, bookings/enrolments/attendance, disruptions, both capacity invariants) · append-only payments/finance entities · twelve state machines with server-rejected invalid transitions · fourteen database invariants (fils, AED, isolation, capacity, idempotency, gateway-event uniqueness, append-only, dual control, ownership FKs, versioning, retention) · ten exact single-transaction boundaries (outbox rows inside; no transaction ever spans a gateway call) · the recoverable checkout/payment saga (explicitly not a distributed transaction) · outbox/inbox rules with the launch event set · the operational server-authorization matrix with PII tiers and child-data minimization · API contract principles (typed results, idempotency, versions/stale conflicts, the docs/09 §22.10 issue-code vocabulary as the platform standard) · a full mock-to-production migration map (**no approved screen requires redesign**; every mock-only field, vocabulary conflict — `Provider`→Organization, free-text categories, `dayOffset`/`MOCK_TODAY`, the dropped `freeTrial` price kind — is named) · seven first backend slices with acceptance criteria, negative-authorization tests, and transaction/concurrency tests · and the §14 owner-decision register (blocking-schema vs blocking-payment/commercial vs configurable values; VAT, commission, recurring billing, cancellation templates, waitlists, multi-participant scope, bundle identifiers, and the gateway are recorded, not decided). The two structural points originally flagged for owner confirmation were **ruled on 2026-08-06 and applied as docs/24 Amendment A1** (documentation-only follow-up commit): **(1)** the first-draft `enrolment_pool` (capacity on RecurringSchedule) was rejected — schedules stay purely temporal rules, and the new **EnrolmentCohort** entity (term/month/intake with program+branch, effective dates, schedule refs, capacity, confirmed/held counts, enrolment cutoff, lifecycle state, price/policy refs) is the canonical third inventory unit; `CapacityHold` targets exactly one of Session, CampWeek, or EnrolmentCohort, all under the identical capacity and capacity-floor invariants, with entity/state-machine/transaction/API/migration/slice-test sections updated accordingly; **(2)** system-initiated refunds are approved **only** where an authoritative event plus a deterministic policy calculation establish the obligation — the system creates the request and calculated amount, normal approval remains a separate Finance action (`initiated_by ≠ approved_by` preserved), automatic approval of ordinary customer/provider refunds is **not** authorized, and gateway voids/automatic same-amount saga reversals are explicitly append-only `PaymentTransaction` reversal postings (auto-executed with audit + reconciliation), never discretionary Refund records. **Stop line honored: no W4 slice planning or implementation starts until the owner gives final approval of docs/24 as amended.**
