## Backend Slice 3 · S3-2 (2026-08-07 — staff memberships, branch scopes, digest-stored invitations; **FULLY APPROVED at `3bcb37d` + guard correction `8784ce0` 2026-08-07**)

**Migration [`0006_staff_memberships_and_invitations.sql`](backend/migrations/0006_staff_memberships_and_invitations.sql)** — exactly the docs/27 §16 row S3-2 scope (Amendment A1/D-S3-1). No provider routes, principal middleware, capability registry, admin lifecycle, storefront read, or listing object exists.

- **`staff_membership`** on the canonical `app_user` (no second identity system): exact 7-role CHECK vocabulary (`owner org_manager branch_manager listings_editor coach front_desk finance`; code↔display mapping in [provider-roles.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/provider-roles.ts)), `active → revoked` terminal machine, append-only history (role/scope change = revoke + NEW row; identity/role/provenance immutable), one-ACTIVE-per-user+org partial unique (multi-org allowed), org-bound composite invitation-provenance FK, `SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE` only.
- **Branch scopes** — `staff_membership_branch` composite-FK'd on BOTH sides with `organization_id` (cross-org scope unrepresentable by direct SQL); org-wide-only roles CHECK-forced to `'all'`; scope rows refused on `'all'` memberships; a `'branches'` membership with an empty set is refused at COMMIT (deferred trigger) — empty can never mean org-wide; scope rows append-only (INSERT+SELECT grants; `forbid_mutation`); branch deactivation never rewrites/transfers scope rows.
- **Last-active-owner guard** — advisory-xact-lock-serialized trigger refuses revoking the final active Owner of a non-offboarded organization (concurrent revocations of the last two Owners leave exactly one, tested); `offboarded` is the sanctioned terminal exception; no owner-transfer flow invented.
- **`staff_invitation`** — `sent → accepted | revoked | expired`; normalized lowercased target email (CHECK); HMAC-SHA-256 `token_digest` + scheme/pepper-version metadata ONLY (B2-6B boundary — raw token exists in service memory + mail payload only, never returned by services, never in rows/logs/audit/outbox — hygiene-tested); overdue rows unacceptable by time alone (trigger) even before the sweep; one live invitation per org+address (partial unique; resend = revoke + new row); target/role/scope/digest/expiry immutable; terminal rows frozen.
- **Services** ([staff-invitations.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/services/staff-invitations.ts), approved S3-2 caller-context abstraction — no fabricated S3-3 middleware): **issue** (owner-membership authority resolved fresh from PostgreSQL, or `admin` context validated as an active `operations` role for the future §13.3 founding path; active-same-org branch validation; resend supersession; audit+outbox in-transaction; post-commit MailSender with `mailDelivery: delivered|failed` on the result — mail failure never rolls back) · **accept** (D-S3-1: verified normalized email match required — unverified/display-name/subject-only/private-relay refused; relay users link+verify via the real Slice-2 flow then succeed; FOR UPDATE + CAS single-use; membership + scope rows + events in ONE §7.10 transaction; all failures byte-identical `invitationInvalid`; suspended/offboarded orgs refuse safely, invitation stays pending) · **revoke** (idempotent, exactly-once events) · **expiry sweep** (B2-5 pattern; deterministic-order locking, concurrent sweeps idempotent, exactly-once transition+events). Events: audit `org.staff_invited/staff_joined/invitation_revoked/invitation_expired`, outbox `staff.invited/joined/invitation_revoked/invitation_expired` on the `organization` aggregate — ids only, email NEVER. Config [staff-invitation-config.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/staff-invitation-config.ts): `HIMMA_STAFF_INVITATION_*` (TTL default 7 days; pepper fails closed in production).
- **Recorded S3-2 notes:** memberships have no expiry state (approved §5 model — time-bounded validity is invitation-side) · membership-revocation service + `org.staff_revoked` emission belong to S3-3 routes (last-owner protection is DB-enforced now) · acceptance/issue refusal on suspended orgs is a service-level application of §7.5 ahead of the S3-3 route policy.

**Structural-secret-guard rule (owner correction, 2026-08-07 — applied after conditional approval of `3bcb37d`):** the S3-2 commit had exempted `*_digest` columns generally from the two Slice-2 structural secret sweeps; the owner replaced that with a **binding clarified rule**: exemption is ONLY by explicit table-qualified allowlist entry in [test/helpers/structural-secrets.ts](backend/test/helpers/structural-secrets.ts) (`SANCTIONED_VERIFIER_COLUMNS`: `audit_event.before_digest/after_digest`, `idempotency_key.request_digest`, `bootstrap_seal.manifest_digest`, `login_session.ip_digest`, `mfa_recovery_code.code_hash`, `staff_invitation.token_digest`). A `_digest`/`_hash` suffix by itself exempts NOTHING: some one-way verifier digests are deliberately stored, but Cognito-managed credentials/tokens/TOTP secrets must never exist in Himma PostgreSQL raw OR hashed — `refresh_token[_digest]`, `password[_hash|_digest]`, `totp_secret[_digest]`, `credential[_digest]`, and provider challenge/session material stay offenders wherever they appear, and an allowlisted column name on any other table stays flagged. [structural-secret-guard.test.ts](backend/test/structural-secret-guard.test.ts) proves: sanctioned columns pass · the four prohibited digest synthetics fail · table-qualification holds (`secret_guard_probe.token_digest` fails) · every allowlist entry must exist in the live schema, so removing/renaming an approved column fails loudly instead of leaving a dormant exemption. Future migrations adding a reviewed digest column must extend the allowlist explicitly (owner-reviewed), never the pattern.

## Backend Slice 3 · S3-3 (2026-08-07 — provider principal, capability registry, and provider-private management routes; **FULLY APPROVED at `051d77c` 2026-08-07**)

No migration — S3-3 is application/HTTP layer only over the approved S3-1/S3-2 schema (schema untouched; codegen zero drift).

- **Capability registry** ([provider-capabilities.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/provider-capabilities.ts)): ACTIVE Slice-3 vocabulary (`org.read` · `org.legal.view` · `commercial_terms.view` · `profile.edit` · `branch.create/edit/deactivate` · `staff.read` · `staff.manage` · `org.submit`) strictly disjoint from the RESERVED docs/27 §6 future vocabulary (listings/schedules/sessions/capacity/offers/media/bookings/reports/attendance/roster/statements/payouts/refund-reports/payout-bank-details + bulk import). Role→active map per §6 (owner = everything incl. staff + submit + commercial-terms sight; org_manager = profile + branches; branch_manager = assigned-branch edit; listings_editor/front_desk/finance = org read; coach = minimized org read). Reserved capabilities are typed separately, never resolved into an orgScope, and the route-registration guard REFUSES a route declaring one — a reserved capability structurally cannot become executable.
- **Provider principal / orgScope** ([provider-principal.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/services/provider-principal.ts)): `RequestPrincipal.orgScope` is now `{ organizationId, membershipId, role, capabilities, branchScope: 'all'|branchIds[], organizationState }`, resolved FRESH from PostgreSQL per request for the ONE addressed `/provider/organizations/:organizationId` (docs/27 §8; nothing org-shaped in tokens per D1/A1.1). No membership, unknown org, and offboarded org collapse into one not-found shape (enumeration posture); Cognito groups/claims-shaped token material is inert (tested); revocation/suspension/role-replacement bite on the next request. `branchScope` lists assigned ACTIVE branches only — deactivation removes reach, empty never falls back to org-wide. `GET /provider/me` lists the caller's memberships (org switcher source).
- **Policies** ([policies.ts](backend/src/modules/identity/http/policies.ts) + [auth-plugin.ts](backend/src/modules/identity/http/auth-plugin.ts)): new `provider` and `providerStepUp` categories in the B2-4 deny-by-default registry. Binding order: live session → fresh membership resolution (404-shaped) → **D-S3-5 MFA baseline** (Himma enrollment + MFA-verified session factor: MFA login or live TOTP/recovery-code grant; `mfaRequired` otherwise) → recent step-up on `providerStepUp` (Slice-2 window; grant or fresh MFA login; `stepUpRequired` when stale) → declared ACTIVE capability (`forbidden` = right org, insufficient role/scope) → suspended-org mutation refusal (`organizationSuspended`; reads still work; offboarded = terminal 404). Provider routes must declare `providerCapability` or registration aborts; a capability on a non-provider policy also aborts. Admin and provider stay disjoint authorities (tested both ways).
- **Routes** ([provider-routes.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/http/provider-routes.ts)) — exactly the docs/27 §13.2 inventory: `GET /provider/me` (authenticatedCustomer) · `GET /provider/organizations/:orgId` (org.read; capability-shaped view — owner sees legal+commercial, managers legal only, coach neither) · `PATCH .../profile` (profile.edit, CAS; publish/unpublish audit vocabulary) · `POST .../submit` (org.submit; draft|rejected→submitted with §10 completeness = display name + ≥1 active branch; typed `organizationIncomplete`/`lifecycleConflict`) · `POST .../branches` + `PATCH .../branches/:id` + `POST .../branches/:id/deactivate` (branch.create/edit/deactivate, branch-scope-checked, CAS, idempotent deactivate) · `GET .../staff` (staff.read; memberships incl. history + invitations) · `POST .../staff/invitations` + `POST .../staff/invitations/:id/revoke` + `POST .../staff/memberships/:id/revoke` (providerStepUp + staff.manage — the D-S3-5 higher-risk set; role/scope change = revoke + re-invite, no owner-transfer flow) · `POST /provider/invitations/accept` (authenticatedCustomer; S3-2 D-S3-1 service). Explicit TypeBox schemas everywhere; no generic all-columns endpoint; last-owner trigger refusals translate to typed `lastOwnerProtected` with no constraint/trigger names in any payload.
- **New typed outcomes:** `organizationSuspended` (403) · `lastOwnerProtected` (409) · `invitationInvalid` (400) · `organizationIncomplete` (409) · `lifecycleConflict` (409) · `invalidBranchScope` (422), joining the existing vocabulary (forbidden/notFound/staleVersion/mfaRequired/stepUpRequired/rateLimited).
- **Rate limits** (existing store abstraction; in-memory dev/test, production store still fail-closed): `staffInvitation` 10/min and `staffManagement` 20/min per issuer+organization digest, `invitationAccept` 10/min per user.
- **Production gate (no bypass):** the provider surface shares the identity MFA-activation capability gate — in production it registers ONLY when the B2-6C `AdminProductionReadiness` capabilities (Cognito integration, MFA provider validation, real-pool SOFTWARE_TOKEN_MFA smoke, approved production config) ALL report ready; forcing `enableProviderRoutes` while unready refuses startup loudly (tested). Dev/test exercise full behavior through deterministic adapters.

## Backend Slice 3 · S3-4 (2026-08-07 — Himma-admin organization lifecycle + customer-public storefront read; **FULLY APPROVED at `4ff461a` 2026-08-07**)

No migration — S3-4 is application/HTTP layer only over the approved S3-1/S3-2 schema (codegen zero drift). Two new surfaces, strictly separated from the S3-3 provider-private surface.

- **Admin creation** ([organization-admin.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/services/organization-admin.ts) + [organization-admin-routes.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/http/organization-admin-routes.ts)): `POST /admin/organizations` (existing `admin` policy — live session + MFA + recent factor — with the `operations` role re-resolved fresh inside the transaction; other admin roles, providers, and customers refused) creates draft organization + public-profile shell (display name defaults from trade name) + the FOUNDING Owner invitation in ONE transaction via the S3-2 invitation model (shared `prepareInvitationToken`/`persistInvitationInTrx`/`composeInvitationMail` helpers — one code path with S3-3 issuance). Raw token exists only in memory + the post-commit mail; forced failure rolls back org/profile/invitation/audit/outbox together and sends nothing; mail failure never rolls back and reports `mailDelivery: 'failed'`; no placeholder User is created for an unknown invitee.
- **Lifecycle administration**: `POST /admin/organizations/:orgId/verification/(start-review|verify|reject)` and `/(go-live|suspend|reinstate|offboard)` — exactly the §5.1 admin edges over the untouched S3-1 trigger (services pre-check into typed outcomes; the trigger stays the authority). Every transition: `operations`-only, CAS on version (`staleVersion`), wrong state → `lifecycleConflict`, unknown org → `notFound`, audit + outbox atomic with the change (`org.review_started/verified/rejected/went_live/suspended/reinstated/offboarded` ↔ `organization.*`), suspended_at/offboarded_at maintained per the state-tie CHECKs, optional bounded `reasonCode` slug (pattern-validated; outbox payload only — free text refused, private notes impossible). Offboarding rides the admin policy's step-up-recency baseline; downstream booking/refund/payout consequences are recorded as FUTURE consumers of the lifecycle events, not invented now.
- **D-S3-3 verification-evidence gate (binding, no bypass):** `verificationEvidenceCapabilityReady` defaults FALSE with no env shortcut; in production `verify` and `go-live` fail closed (typed 503 `verificationEvidenceUnavailable` + audited attempt `org.verification_gate_refused`, org state unmoved) — and because Slice 3 ships NO VerificationCase/document-review implementation, a production build claiming readiness REFUSES STARTUP (build-app throws; the flag becomes honestly settable only when the admin workstream lands the real capability). Dev/test exercise the full lifecycle deterministically; ungated edges (start-review, reject, suspend…) work in production; admin roles grant no bypass (tested with a fully-activated production admin surface).
- **Customer-public storefront** ([storefront-read.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/services/storefront-read.ts) + [storefront-routes.ts](backend/src/modules/provider/http/storefront-routes.ts)): `GET /providers/:organizationId` (explicit `public` policy; no auth of any kind) serving the §13.1 projection ONLY — display name, en/ar descriptions, media refs, derived `verified` badge (live ⇒ true, never stored), the four explicit public contacts, and ACTIVE branches (id/label/addressLine/areaLabel/geo/openingHours/facilities) in stable creation (UUIDv7 id) order. Effective visibility = `verification_state='live' AND published=true`; draft/submitted/in_review/verified-not-live/rejected/suspended/offboarded/unpublished/nonexistent are byte-identical 404s. **Structural projection lock (§14.9):** `PUBLIC_STOREFRONT_SOURCES` is a closed, test-asserted column list (organization contributes id + eligibility state only; `published`/`active` are predicates, never serialized); no `select *` exists; the test adds a scratch private column with a sentinel value and proves it cannot appear — widening the projection is an owner-visible diff. Listings/ratings deliberately ABSENT (no fake placeholder arrays): Slice 4 composes its listing read models WITH this contract. Conservative HTTP semantics: schema-rejected malformed ids, nosniff, no invented cache/CDN contract (storefront cache headers stay a docs/27 §17 configurable for later).
- **Wiring:** admin org routes register inside the existing admin-surface production capability gate; the public storefront registers with the identity surface everywhere (no MFA dependency). New typed outcome: `verificationEvidenceUnavailable` (503).

**Verification (all green, 2026-08-07):** backend tsc clean · ESLint clean · backend Jest **493/493 across 42 suites** (7 new [organization-admin](backend/test/organization-admin.test.ts): atomic creation + digest-only + no-placeholder-user + post-commit mail · operations-only authority incl. access_admin/provider/customer refusals · forced-failure full rollback with sanitized 500 · mail-failure persistence · full lifecycle walk with CAS/events/terminal refusal · invalid/stale/unknown typed outcomes · reason-code slug + free-text refusal + resubmission edge · suspension biting provider mutations + storefront with reinstatement restore and intact history · the D-S3-3 matrix (startup refusal on claimed readiness; production verify AND go-live 503 + audited attempt with state unmoved on a FULLY-activated admin surface; ungated edges still work); 7 new [storefront-public](backend/test/storefront-public.test.ts): live+published serves, all 8 ineligible shapes byte-identical 404 · no-auth + garbage-bearer-ignored + malformed-id 422 + conservative headers · exact projection key sets + active-branch filtering/ordering + no inactive leak · private/staff/invitation/security data absent under a forbidden-pattern scan · structural source-list lock + scratch-private-column leak proof) · two S3-3 route-filter assertions intentionally updated for the new public `/providers/…` surface (`/provider/` prefix) · route-policy snapshot intentionally updated (+10 entries: 8 admin org routes + public storefront GET/HEAD) · migrate-from-zero + `db:verify` green · codegen zero drift · all S3-1…S3-3 and Slice-1/Slice-2 suites green.

## W2-10 · Provider Bulk Import UX (2026-08-14 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `24b385a` (2026-08-14); owner ruling D-W2-3 ratified at closure**)

**Scope delivered (docs/23 §8.5 rules 1–5; docs/29 §16 W2-10, PP-17):** the production-quality bulk-import PREVIEW at `/o/:organizationId/listings/import` (docs/29 §6 route; role-aware "Import listings from a spreadsheet" entry on the Listings index; no new navigation item). **The import ENGINE does not exist and NOTHING here pretends it does:** no file leaves the browser, no endpoint is called or invented, no import job/ID exists, no listing is created/submitted/published, and the truthful end state is *"ready for import once bulk processing is enabled — nothing has been imported yet."* Production stays fail-closed (unconfigured `BulkImportPort` refuses; default build green). §8.5 rule 2 (bulk scheduling) awaits the Slice-6 backend; rule 6 (batch media) is excluded per docs/29 (Class-C media gap carried).

**Import seam ([catalogue/import-contract.ts](portal/src/catalogue/import-contract.ts) `BulkImportPort`):** exactly ONE operation — `dryRun(organizationId, rows)` → `dryRunComplete{report} | forbidden | notFound | organizationSuspended | unavailable`. **No execute/commit operation exists** (no canonical backend contract does — structural lock test sweeps executeImport/commitBatch/createImportJob and friends). The fixture implementation runs the policy pipeline every catalogue mutation workflow uses (`listings.manage` → suspended refusal → transient control), then validates against the SHARED fixture truth — ACTIVE taxonomy, the org's branches, the caller's branch scope, and the caller's REACHABLE listing titles (a Branch Manager never receives out-of-scope titles through duplicate warnings — test-proven). **Dry runs are READ-ONLY: index/detail truth proven deep-equal before and after a run; no draft appears, nothing is auto-submitted/published (task §§25–27).** The live W2-12+ implementation becomes a server-side dry-run once that contract exists (Class B recorded).

**Format truth (task §4):** CSV + XLSX are the §8.5-approved production capabilities, but the PRODUCTION template/column contract is canonically UNRESOLVED — docs/23 §18.17 explicitly blocks the §8.5 template on the Arabic-content workflow decision, and no column specification exists anywhere in the repository. The tool therefore runs on the clearly versioned **"import preview format v1"** (labelled in the UI as a working format), parses **CSV in the browser** (hand-rolled RFC-4180: quotes, embedded commas/newlines, CRLF, BOM; NUL-sniffed binary rejection; bounded at 512 KB / 500 data rows / 40 columns — UX-only limits, recorded as not the server contract), and **refuses .xlsx/.xls with save-as-CSV guidance** (production XLSX parsing arrives with the engine; recorded). No other formats.

**v1 fixture schema (D-S4-1 made visible):** one ROW = one ProgramPriceOption; rows sharing a non-blank `listing_ref` group into ONE listing — never one listing per option. 18 columns mirroring the real create-body + branches + price options (title_en\*, activity_type\*, setting\*, who_for\* required; Arabic optional as ever; offers/media deliberately absent — canon never names them in the import format, recorded rather than silently added). Continuation rows may leave listing-level fields blank (they inherit); a non-blank mismatch is a per-row/field error. **CREATE-only** (docs/24 §2.8: import-created listings are ALWAYS `draft`; no update/matching semantics are approved — none invented, no title matching, no fuzzy anything). Downloadable v1 template (header + a worked two-row grouping example) and the §8.5 rule-4 per-row validation-report CSV are both spreadsheet-formula-injection guarded (leading `=`/`+`/`-`/`@` neutralized).

**Column mapping (§8.5 rule 1):** auto-match on normalized headers (template header or display label; no fuzzy matching), per-field remapping selects (uniqueness enforced), ignored-columns note, and required-fields-unmapped guidance instead of a doomed run.

**Dry-run validation ([catalogue/import-validation.ts](portal/src/catalogue/import-validation.ts) — pure, port-injected reference truth):** mirrors the REAL catalogue semantics, deliberately neither stricter nor weaker: exact TypeBox text limits (160/4000/1000/120) · English title required · ACTIVE taxonomy by exact case-insensitive label · five-value gender vocabulary (provider labels AND canonical codes) · age ties (0–130 ints, min≤max, all-ages exclusivity) · same-organization ACTIVE branch resolution by exact portal name (unknown/deactivated/ambiguous refuse; cross-org unrepresentable by construction; Branch-Manager out-of-scope branches refuse with the association rule's language) · S4-1 price ties through the PROVEN W2-8 AED⇄fils boundary (19.99 → 1999 fils exact; free⇔no amount, paid⇒positive amount, package⇔sessions) · price details without a price type refuse. **Severity model:** error (row cannot import) / warning (review) / info (guidance); warnings never block (test-proven). **§8.5 rule-5 duplicates WARN-only, never merged, never blocking:** in-file same-title across different listings, repeated option shapes within one listing, and same-title matches against the caller's current catalogue (titles are all the list contract exposes — branch-level live comparison needs the engine, recorded). **Atomicity (§8.5 rules 3–4) stated truthfully:** a file with errors imports nothing; *"Continue with the N valid rows"* forms an explicit NEW previewed batch (re-validated subset, original row numbers kept). Listing preview notes state draft truth ("created as a private draft… Himma review still applies") and completeness consequences (no option/branch yet → incomplete-draft guidance) without inventing frontend completeness rules.

**Authority (task §6):** capability = `listings.manage` — Owner, Organization Manager, Listings Editor (docs/23 grants LE "bulk import previews"; W2-10 IS preview), Branch Manager with the scope named up front ("Your branch scope: … rows that reference other branches won't validate") and enforced per-row; coach/front-desk/finance get a truthful no-access surface with no entry point; suspended organizations get no upload surface (mutation workflow). Authoritative branch-scoped import validation for the future engine is a recorded Class-B requirement.

**UX flow:** instructions ("Before you start": draft truth, atomicity, engine status, format status, template download, collapsible column guidance) → file selection (labelled input + drag-drop alternative — fully keyboard operable; file stays on-device) → file-level failures in provider words (unsupported type, Excel-not-yet, empty, header-only, unreadable/binary, unclosed quote, duplicate columns, too many rows/columns, ragged rows with row numbers) → column matching → validation preview (summary tiles: rows read / validate / need fixes / double-check / listings detected / options detected · verdict alert · "What this batch creates" listing cards · "Rows needing attention" cards with severity chips + field + provider-readable reason, errors traceable to row and field) → error-report download · valid-subset re-batch · remove/replace resets safely (mapping changes invalidate stale reports). Focus moves to the results region after a run; a polite status region announces running/complete; hostile cell content (formula/HTML) renders strictly as text (test-proven).

**Verification (all green this commit):** portal tsc clean · ESLint `--max-warnings=0` clean · **Jest 586/586 across 51 suites** (11 file/parsing tests: RFC-4180 incl. quotes/CRLF/BOM, every file-level failure, bounds, binary sniff, padding, auto-map/normalization, required-column reporting, row numbering, formula guard, template lock to the schema headers, error-report shape · 11 semantic-validation tests: required/optional truth incl. Arabic, taxonomy, branch matrix incl. ambiguity + cross-org + BM scope, eligibility matrix, exact-fils money, price ties, D-S4-1 grouping + continuation + conflicts, duplicate warn-never-block matrix, mixed-file summary truth · 8 port tests: dryRun-only structural lock + fail-closed + seven-role authority + suspended/notFound/transient + BM scope + reachable-titles-only + the READ-ONLY deep-equal proof + no-fabricated-persistence sweep · 10 page tests: no-access + no entry point, authorized journey, suspended, BM scope note, valid end-to-end with fetch-spy no-network + no-fake-vocabulary sweeps, shared-store-untouched, mixed/atomicity/row-field errors + subset re-batch, file-failure recovery + replace-resets, unmapped-required gating, malicious-cell-as-text · 5 jest-axe import states — zero violations · all W2-1…W2-9 suites preserved untouched) · **Playwright 193 passed / 8 by-design skips** at 1440/1024/390 over the production fixture build (15 new W2-10 tests: owner journey incl. catalogue-untouched proof, mixed + subset re-batch, blocked file recovery, BM scope, no-access; zero console errors asserted per test) · **default production build green and fail-closed**. Root/customer app untouched; **no backend change (no PostgreSQL certification applies)**. **Screenshot evidence: `artifacts/portal-w2-10/`** (21 captures — 7 states × 3 viewports).

1. **Class A (owner/product):** the production import file contract — template columns (already blocked by docs/23 §18.17 Arabic decision), the `listing_ref` grouping representation, taxonomy/branch matching semantics (v1 exact-label match is a working rule, not ratified), and create-vs-update evolution. v1 is explicitly pre-release.

Nothing was invented to fill these.

## W2-9 · Provider Listing Lifecycle, Moderation & Revision UX + Dashboard (2026-08-14 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `654be5c` (2026-08-14)**)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-9, docs/29 §10 dashboard):** the provider-facing LIFECYCLE surface over the exact S4 named actions, the moderation/revision state UX, and the first real Dashboard — all behind fixture ports; NO admin decision UI, NO live integration, NO backend change. **Deliberately absent (later tasks/canon):** internal moderation/revision decisions (W3) · bulk import (W2-10) · schedules/bookings/attendance/finance/reports · real API calls (W2-12) · any restore/reactivation of archived listings, provider revision withdrawal, or reviewer feedback display (no such contracts exist).

**Lifecycle seam ([catalogue/lifecycle-contract.ts](portal/src/catalogue/lifecycle-contract.ts) `ListingLifecyclePort`):** EXACTLY the four real S4 lifecycle routes, operation for operation — `submitProgram` ⇄ POST …/submit (`listings.manage`) · `publishProgram` ⇄ POST …/publish · `pauseProgram` ⇄ POST …/pause · `archiveProgram` ⇄ POST …/archive (all three `listings.publish`); every body is `{expectedVersion}` CAS. **No separate resume/unpublish route exists — resume IS publish from `paused`, pause IS unpublish; resubmit IS submit from `changes_requested`; archive returns no version (terminal).** Outcome vocabulary and per-action check order mirror the services verbatim: submit → notFound → branch-scope `forbidden` (the stricter `every`-rule, applied to submit exactly as the backend does) → lifecycleConflict (draft|changes_requested only) → staleVersion → `programIncomplete{missing[]}`; publish → notFound → lifecycleConflict (approved|paused only) → `organizationNotLive` (verification_state ≠ `live`) → staleVersion → completeness (re-checked at publish, `publishedAt` stamped only on first publication from approved); pause (published only) and archive (published|paused only) → notFound → lifecycleConflict → staleVersion. Publish/pause/archive consult NO branch scope (no scoped role holds `listings.publish` — the unions carry no scope refusal, mirroring the backend asymmetry). Policy pipeline ahead of everything: org/membership not-found shaping → capability `forbidden` → suspended-organization mutation refusal → transient-failure control. The port lives beside (never inside) the W2-8 editor port: the 13-operation editor lock stands untouched, and the structural lifecycle lock pins exactly these four operations. Unconfigured production port fails CLOSED on all four (test-proven; default production build green and fail-closed).

**One action home (task §16):** the listing detail's STATUS section hosts the [ListingLifecyclePanel](portal/src/pages/listings/listing-lifecycle-panel.tsx) — the only place lifecycle commands exist. The editor links there (readiness panel when complete; changes-requested banner copy; revision-pending link) and never carries a command (the W2-8 sweep was NARROWED, not discarded: the editor sweep still proves zero lifecycle commands there; the detail sweep now applies to unauthorized viewers and Himma-owned states). Actions derive from ONE pure function ([lifecycle-domain.ts](portal/src/pages/listings/lifecycle-domain.ts) `lifecyclePlan`): draft/changes_requested + `listings.manage` + mutable scope + COMPLETE → Submit/Resubmit for review (an incomplete listing is never offered a submit the backend would refuse — the readiness list explains what's missing; the port still models `programIncomplete`, and a race-time server refusal renders the exact structured `missing[]`); approved/paused + `listings.publish` + org live + complete → Publish listing / Resume publishing (+ Archive from paused); published + `listings.publish` → Pause listing · Archive listing; suspended orgs, submitted/in_review (Himma owns the next step), and archived (terminal) offer nothing. **No disabled-theater anywhere:** viewers without authority get truthful explanations — approved for Listings Editor/Branch Manager says "Himma approved this listing… an Owner or Organization Manager publishes it" (task §17, test-proven); Branch-Manager-readable-but-not-mutable listings show readiness truth with zero controls (read ≠ mutate, proven at port and UI level).

**Command discipline (task §§10–12):** pending state + busy labels; ONE in-flight command (duplicate activation executes once — spy-proven); success announced ONLY after the port confirms, in a focus-managed alert region; confirmations only where they help — pause (names the public-visibility consequence, reversible) and archive (destructive dialog: "final and can't be reversed", terminal, no Restore anywhere) — submit/publish/resume run without confirmation dialogs. CAS: a stale command NEVER overwrites — the panel reconciles current truth (query invalidation), tells the provider the listing changed, and requires a deliberate retry (which then operates on fresh truth); lifecycleConflict and notFound reconcile the same way. `organizationNotLive` renders as the ORGANIZATION-level gate (own copy + link to the W2-3 onboarding/verification surface), never as listing incompleteness (task §20, test-proven distinct); no publish control is offered while the org isn't live. Raw version numbers never render.

**Moderation-state UX (task §§7–8):** provider-facing treatments for all four review states — submitted ("Waiting for Himma's review"), in_review ("Himma is reviewing"), approved ("approval and publication are separate steps — it stays off the catalogue until an Owner or Organization Manager publishes"), changes_requested (attention chip + "needs your attention and a new submission" + Resubmit path). Submission never implies approval; approval never implies publication. **No reviewer feedback is displayed or invented — re-proven from the repository this task: the admin `reasonCode` (bounded slug, never free text) is written ONLY into the audit/outbox event payload (moderation.ts), no program/program_revision column stores it, and no provider-facing schema carries any reason/message field. The Class-B provider-feedback contract gap stands and is carried forward for W2-12/backend planning.**

**ProgramRevision provider UX (task §9):** the [revision STATUS page](portal/src/pages/listings/listing-revision-page.tsx) at `/o/:organizationId/listings/:programId/revision` (docs/29 §6's "diff view" honestly reduced to a STATUS view — the provider contract exposes exactly `openRevision {id, state('submitted'|'in_review'), createdAt, version}`, no change-set content, so no truthful diff can exist; recorded Class-A gap). It renders: pending state ("Waiting for review"/"Being reviewed") + submitted date + live-values-stand truth + protected-changes-locked note + "nothing you need to do"; with no open revision, the quiet truth that a decided revision clears from the provider view (approved changes appear in the listing; no fabricated history/decision detail — the provider cannot distinguish approve from reject and the UI never pretends to). ZERO decision controls (no approve/reject/cancel/withdraw — test-swept). Detail and editor link to it; protected controls stay locked while a revision is open (W2-8 behavior unchanged).

**Dashboard (docs/29 §10; task §§14–15, 21):** the real first Dashboard at `/o/:organizationId` ([dashboard-page.tsx](portal/src/pages/dashboard/dashboard-page.tsx)), replacing the W2-1 placeholder — derived ONLY from backend-ready truth: **Organization card** (provider-language verification state, storefront publication line, link → onboarding while still onboarding / profile once live; suspended copy for suspended orgs) · **Listings card** (`catalogue.read` holders only; counts by REAL lifecycle state composed client-side by walking the real paginated listings read at limit 100 — no aggregate/counts endpoint exists, recorded Class-A composition note; actionable rows: changes-requested, approved-awaiting-publication with role-aware wording (publish-capable vs "an Owner or Organization Manager publishes them"), drafts; "Nothing needs your attention" when clean; links into the listings workspace + role-aware Create) · **Team card** (Owner-only `staff.read`: active members + invitations awaiting a response). A Branch Manager's counts cover exactly the scoped reachable set the backend serves (never org-wide — leak-proven-absent); roles without catalogue access get an org-status-only dashboard with ZERO listing/staff fetches (spy-proven). **Deliberately absent: revision counts (the list summary rows carry no revision field — composing would need N detail calls; not faked) and every future-domain metric (bookings/revenue/GMV/payouts/attendance/conversion/utilization/capacity/ratings/customer counts — regex-swept in Jest AND Playwright).** States: per-card loading, PARTIAL failure (a catalogue load failure degrades one card with retry while the rest stands), true-empty catalogue with creation entry, scope-limited role, workspace failure with retry. Lifecycle mutations refresh the dashboard through shared query invalidation (no second truth source — proven before/after in Jest and e2e).

**Fixtures:** the lifecycle port operates on the SAME shared per-organization catalogue store as W2-7/W2-8 (no fork); mutations bump version/updatedAt so index, detail, editor, and dashboard see fresh truth immediately. Existing controls model the outcome vocabulary (`failNextListingMutation` → unavailable; `simulateConcurrentListingEdit` → staleVersion; no new control needed). New fixture: **Pearl Divers Freediving** (verified + published storefront, NOT live) gains the complete APPROVED listing `pearlFreediving` — the truthful `organizationNotLive` case (the block is the organization, not the listing). Blue Wave's 12-state catalogue, Noor's complete draft (`noorExamPrep`), and the suspended Falcon published listing exercise the rest.

Branch Manager every-rule read≠mutate construction, publication authority Owner+OrgMgr-only with forbidden for LE/BM, full state-machine walk incl. approval-never-auto-publishes + ## W2-8 · Provider Listing Editor UX (2026-08-08 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `c65ca15` (2026-08-14)**)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-8):** the final provider LISTING EDITOR at `/o/:organizationId/listings/new` (creation) and `/o/:organizationId/listings/:programId/edit` (editing) — the management surface over the SAME shared catalogue truth the W2-7 read surfaces serve (W2-7's detail stays read-oriented and now carries the role-aware "Edit listing" entry; the index gains the role-aware "Create listing" entry, superseding the W2-7 "arrives in an upcoming update" empty-state copy and the phantom `/listings/new` not-found). **Deliberately absent (later tasks/backends):** ALL lifecycle actions (W2-9: submit, resubmit, publish, unpublish, pause, resume, archive-listing), the full ProgramRevision workflow/queue/diff UX and `listings/:programId/revision` route (W2-9), moderation decisions, bulk import (W2-10), schedules/sessions/capacity/bookings/payments, media upload/storage, customer-app integration, real API calls, backend changes, W2-12 integration. Test-proven: no lifecycle vocabulary on any editor surface (button/link sweeps + Playwright), the readiness panel is information-only (zero buttons), and the port type structurally cannot express a lifecycle action.

**Mutation seam ([catalogue/editor-contract.ts](portal/src/catalogue/editor-contract.ts) `ListingEditorPort`):** EXACTLY the 13 real S4 mutation operations, mirrored route-for-route (capability in the real registry: `listings.manage` everywhere except media's `media.manage`): `createProgram` ⇄ POST /listings · `updateProgram` ⇄ PATCH (CAS, sensitive routing) · price options add/PATCH/archive (add carries no CAS — the real body has none; PATCH/archive CAS on option version; reorder IS `sortHint` PATCH — no bulk endpoint exists so the UI renumbers via per-option PATCHes) · branch association add/remove (no CAS — real bodies carry none; re-association reactivates the historical row) · media add/PATCH/archive · offer add/PATCH/end (PATCH cannot change `kind` — the real body has none). Exact outcome vocabulary per operation (`programCreated/programUpdated/revisionSubmitted{appliedFields,deferredFields}/revisionPending/optionAdded/optionUpdated/optionArchived/branchAssociated/branchAssociationRemoved/mediaAdded/mediaUpdated/mediaArchived/offerAdded/offerUpdated/offerEnded | invalidTaxonomy | invalidEligibility | invalidPriceOption | invalidBranch | invalidOffer | lifecycleConflict | staleVersion | revisionPending | forbidden | notFound | organizationSuspended | unavailable`), refusal ORDER mirroring the policy pipeline (not-found shaping → capability forbidden → suspended-mutation → service outcomes). Unconfigured production port fails CLOSED on all 13 operations (test-proven; default production build green and fail-closed).

**Edit-state matrix (docs/24 §5.3, exact backend `editModeOf`):** `draft`/`changes_requested` edit DIRECTLY (changes-requested carries the correction banner; resubmission truthfully named as W2-9) · `approved`/`published`/`paused` are REVIEW-GATED: non-sensitive fields (titleEn/titleAr/setting/activityTypeId) + branch associations + media + offers apply directly (hot-apply, exactly like the backend), while the docs/28 §7 sensitive set (descriptions, eligibility fields, and EVERY price-option operation) routes through a ProgramRevision — the editor marks protected fields ("Needs Himma review"), names the boundary before saving ("Send for review" on gated option forms), announces `appliedFields`/`deferredFields` in provider words after, and the LIVE values stand (test-proven at port and UI level) · at most ONE open revision: while one is pending the protected fields render read-only with honest copy (the backend would refuse `revisionPending`) · `submitted`/`in_review`/`archived` render a FROZEN read-only editor state (truthful copy per state, zero form controls, zero disabled-theater, link to the read view); archived is explicitly final.

**Authority (the REAL registry + the REAL branch-scope mutation rule):** create/edit = owner, org_manager, listings_editor org-wide; branch_manager within the STRICTER mutation rule (`programInBranchScope` `every`-semantics — untouched by the W2-7 read correction, mirrored as `listingMutableForScope`): a listing is BM-editable only while every active association is inside the assigned ACTIVE branches; a readable-but-not-mutable listing (some-rule reach) renders a truthful scope surface with ZERO form controls — **read access never implies mutation access (test-proven at port and UI level)**; out-of-read-scope ids get the same safe not-found surface on the editor route. BM branch association controls exist only for assigned branches ("Outside your branch scope" otherwise, backend `forbidden` mirrored). Coach/front_desk/finance: no mutation anywhere, no fetches, truthful no-access surface. Suspended organizations: policy-layer `organizationSuspended` on every mutation; the editor renders read-only with the canonical banner and no mutation controls; the create route refuses. Entry points (index Create, detail Edit) appear only with true authority.

**Creation (S4 drafts may be incomplete):** the create form asks only the real structural fields (English title, ACTIVE-taxonomy activity type, setting, five-value gender vocabulary; optional descriptions/eligibility) — no completeness gate, no wizard; a created draft starts at `draft` v1, lands in its editor, and is immediately in the SHARED store the index/detail/onboarding read. No auto-submission, no publication. Client zod mirrors the exact TypeBox limits (160/4000/1000, ages 0–130, cross-field age rules); Arabic never blocks.

**Money (§29):** [catalogue/money.ts](portal/src/catalogue/money.ts) — deterministic AED⇄fils at the adapter boundary: strict digit-text parsing (≤2 decimals, positive, bounded; commas/symbols/exponents refused with named reasons), exact integer arithmetic (no parseFloat, no float money anywhere; `19.99 → 1999` proven against the float trap), exact round-trip `filsToAedInput`; package sessions-count parsing. Heavily unit-tested (10 tests). Free/paid/package kind ties enforced client-side AND by the fixture's exact `optionShapeValid` mirror; no `Program.price` exists anywhere (key-set locked since W2-7).

**CAS & concurrency (§23):** program PATCH, option PATCH/archive, media PATCH/archive, offer PATCH/end all carry `expectedVersion`; stale writers NEVER overwrite (port-proven for program/option/media/offer). The details form reconciles fresher truth with `keepDirtyValues` (untouched fields adopt the latest saved values, the user's edits stay) and the stale-conflict surface offers "Reload latest version" preserving user values for review-and-resave (UI-proven, e2e-captured); child stale conflicts reload the row with an honest alert. Duplicate submissions execute once (busy-guarded; second same-version write is `staleVersion`; archive/remove/end idempotent exactly like the services). Raw version numbers are never rendered. New fixture controls: `failNextListingMutation` · `simulateConcurrentListingEdit` · `simulateConcurrentPriceOptionEdit`.

**Editor IA:** Details (Basics + Eligibility — ONE dirty-field PATCH per save, "Only the fields you changed are saved."), Locations (associate/remove over the shared W2-5 branch truth; deactivated branches render truthfully and can't newly qualify (`invalidBranch`); a draft with no branch is legitimate), Pricing (D-S4-1: several options under ONE listing; stable ids; deterministic (sortHint, id) order; keyboard-accessible Move up/down reorder — renumbering per-option `sortHint` PATCHes, hidden on review-gated listings where any option change is a review-routed change; archive-only retirement behind an explicit "can never be reactivated" confirmation; archived options stay visible as history with no reactivate control), Photos (reference METADATA only: description edit, ordering, removal; NO add/upload control — see gaps), Offers (exact kinds `freeTrial/paidTrial/discount/promo`; paid-trial amount tie; effective-window validation with device-timezone labeling; end-is-final behind confirmation; ended offers are read-only history; Offer ≠ ProgramPriceOption preserved), plus the readiness panel (draft/changes-requested only: the exact four structured completeness rules as INFORMATION — no Submit exists). Unsaved-changes blocker (the W2-4/W2-5 pattern) on the details form and create flow; child operations are explicit immediate commands and never fake page dirtiness.

## Provider catalogue read-scope correction (2026-08-08 — post-W2-7 conditional approval; **owner-approved with W2-7's closure at `44ac54c`** — see the W2-7 header below)

**Trigger:** W2-7 was CONDITIONALLY APPROVED at `5396366`; the owner ordered ONE bounded correction to the existing provider-private catalogue READ implementation before any W2-8 work — two linked defects W2-7 had exposed and truthfully mirrored.

**Defect 1 — scoped-list pagination truncation (backend, fixed):** `listProviderPrograms` fetched the `limit + 1` window ORGANIZATION-WIDE, then filtered Branch Manager reachability in application memory — so inaccessible rows consumed page slots, a scoped page could return fewer than `limit` rows with a null `nextCursor` while reachable rows existed beyond the window, and the cursor derived from the filtered window. **Corrected:** the reachability predicate now participates in the authoritative Kysely query BEFORE ordering, cursor continuation, and `LIMIT limit + 1`, so keyset pagination walks exactly the reachable ordered set. The fix also surfaced (via the new RED walk test) a latent cursor-precision defect in the same query: the anchor's `created_at` round-tripped through a millisecond-precision JS `Date`, letting a microsecond-timestamped anchor row re-qualify on the next page (duplicate rows); the continuation comparison is now evaluated entirely in SQL (`(created_at, id) > (SELECT created_at, id FROM program WHERE id = cursor)`), preserving the ignored-unknown/foreign-cursor semantics unchanged.

**Defect 2 — Branch Manager detail read was organization-wide (backend, fixed):** `getProviderProgram` applied no branch-scope filter even though the approved Slice-4 model defines Branch Manager catalogue authority as branch-scoped. **Corrected:** ONE canonical reachability rule is now centralized as `programReadableInBranchScope` ([catalogue-shared.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/catalogue-shared.ts)) — reachable = no ACTIVE branch association at all (a draft not yet placed anywhere) OR ≥1 active association to an assigned ACTIVE branch (exactly the already-approved `some`-semantics the scoped list shipped with; NOT invented) — and BOTH reads consume it: the list as a pre-window SQL filter, the detail as an org-scoped reachability precheck. A Branch Manager now reads exactly what their list reaches; an in-organization out-of-scope Program collapses into the SAME `programNotFound` shape as foreign and ghost ids (no enumeration oracle). Owner / Organization Manager / Listings Editor keep their organization-wide catalogue read; Coach / Front Desk / Finance keep zero catalogue access; mutation authority (the stricter `every`-rule `programInBranchScope`, publication matrix, MFA policy) is UNTOUCHED. No migration was needed (query logic only).

**Regression coverage (RED-first, real PostgreSQL — [catalogue-branch-scope-read.test.ts](backend/test/catalogue-branch-scope-read.test.ts), 10 tests):** interleaved reachable/unreachable dataset crossing multiple pages at limit 3 (the old code demonstrably truncated: 2-row page, dead cursor) — first page fills with reachable rows only · full walk returns every reachable Program exactly once, in order, no duplicates, no unreachable row, terminal page correct · org-wide walk unaffected (13/13, duplicate-free) · empty branch scope grants only branchless drafts (never org-wide fallback) · detail: in-scope/mixed/branchless readable; out-of-scope ≡ foreign ≡ ghost (deep-equal `programNotFound`) · detail readability ≡ list reachability for every case · org-wide roles read out-of-scope details unchanged · scope changes bite immediately from database truth: losing the qualifying association removes reach (and a now-branchless draft is reachable again), branch deactivation bites through fresh `resolveOrgScope` resolution, org-wide readers unaffected throughout.

**Portal reconciliation (fixture/UX mirror of the corrected contract — no W2-7 redesign):** the fixture list now filters reachability BEFORE windowing and `loadListing` shares the same seat-reachability helper; the W2-7 out-of-scope detail experience ("runs only at branches outside your assigned branches… you can still read it here") is REMOVED — a Branch Manager's direct URL to an out-of-scope in-org listing now renders the byte-identical safe "Listing not found" surface (test-proven identical to unknown/foreign) — and the now-dead `listingReachableForScope` display helper was deleted. The scoped index truthfully gains the previously-truncated reachable listing (Aqua Fitness Express: 8 reachable rows, not 7). Contract docs ([catalogue/contract.ts](portal/src/catalogue/contract.ts)) updated to the corrected semantics; the W2-7 recorded-defect mirror tests were replaced by corrected-contract tests (pre-window scope pagination walk; detail≡list reachability; org-wide readers unchanged). One pre-existing lint blocker at `5396366` (unused `ADULT_SWIMMING_ID` in [listings.spec.ts](portal/e2e/listings.spec.ts)) was removed to restore the required clean-ESLint gate. W2-7 is otherwise UNCHANGED — no editor, no mutation surface, no new routes.

**Verification (all green this commit):** backend tsc clean · ESLint clean · **backend Jest 682/682 across 55 suites** (incl. the 10 new regression tests; route-policy inventory/snapshot unchanged) · `db:verify` green (10 migrations applied, 0 pending; schema + checksum verification passed) · codegen zero drift · **portal Jest 410/410 across 37 suites** · portal tsc clean · portal ESLint `--max-warnings=0` clean · portal production build green · **Playwright W2-7 listings flows 15/15** at 1440/1024/390 (scoped index incl. Aqua Fitness Express; out-of-scope detail → safe not-found). No native/customer-app change; no recertification triggered.

**Superseded records:** W2-7 contract-gap #1 (pagination truncation) and the "DETAIL read is organization-scoped ONLY" branch-scope description in the W2-7 section below are historical records of the defective behavior this correction fixed — the paragraphs stand as history; this section is the current truth. **W2-8 remains NOT started (awaiting owner instruction).**

## W2-7 · Provider Listings Index & Detail UX (2026-08-08 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED (2026-08-08): conditionally approved at `5396366`, closed including the ordered catalogue-read-scope correction at `44ac54c`** — branch-scope semantics and recorded gap #1 below are superseded by that correction; W2-8 later replaced the empty-state "creation arrives in an upcoming update" copy and the phantom `/listings/new` not-found with the real creation entry points)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-7):** the final READ-ORIENTED Listings experience at the approved routes `/o/:organizationId/listings` (index) · `/o/:organizationId/listings/:programId` (detail), replacing the W2-1 placeholder. **Deliberately absent (later tasks/backends):** listing creation/editor and every mutation (W2-8: create/PATCH, ProgramPriceOption/ProgramBranch/media/offer mutation) · lifecycle actions and revision submission (W2-9: submit/publish/pause/resume/archive, revision UX, dashboard) · `listings/new` and `listings/:programId/revision` routes (they belong structurally to W2-8/W2-9; a stray `/listings/new` URL safely resolves to the not-found shape) · bulk import (W2-10) · schedules/sessions/capacity/bookings/attendance/finance/analytics · admin moderation · real API calls · backend changes · W2-12 integration. Test-proven: zero mutation controls on either surface (button/textbox/combobox/file-input sweeps), no fake marketplace truth anywhere (prohibited-vocabulary sweep: no bookings/capacity/spots/sessions-schedule/attendance/ratings/reviews/revenue/conversion/popularity/payout/payment), and no Listing network calls.

**Catalogue read truth (exactly the closed S4 backend):** TWO reads only, mirrored field-for-field in [catalogue/contract.ts](portal/src/catalogue/contract.ts) (`ListingsReadPort`, structurally locked to `listListings`+`loadListing`): `GET /provider/organizations/:orgId/listings` (capability `catalogue.read`; summary rows carry EXACTLY `id/titleEn/listingState/activityTypeId/version/createdAt/updatedAt` — no price, branch, media, offer, revision, or operational field exists on the list contract, so the index truthfully shows title + lifecycle chip + activity label + updated date and nothing else) and `GET .../listings/:programId` (the full ProgramDetailView incl. embedded activityType, eligibility, priceOptions, branch associations with `branchActive`/`associationActive`, media reference metadata, offers, `openRevision`). Activity labels for the index come from the real public taxonomy read (`GET /catalogue/activity-types` ⇄ new `ActivityTypeReadPort` in [taxonomy/contract.ts](portal/src/taxonomy/contract.ts), ACTIVE rows only); the detail needs no lookup (its embedded activityType carries the truthful `active` flag). Both ports fail closed unconfigured (test-proven; default production build green and fail-closed).

**Pagination (exact wire semantics):** keyset in `(createdAt, id)` order; `limit` 1–100 (portal pages at 10); `nextCursor` is an OPAQUE listing id; an unknown/foreign cursor is ignored exactly like the shipped service (list restarts); "Load more" appends pages (TanStack useInfiniteQuery), and a failed further page keeps loaded rows with a retryable inline error. **Recorded backend observation (mirrored, NOT worked around):** `listProviderPrograms` applies the branch-scope filter AFTER fetching the `limit+1` window, so a branch-scoped caller's page can silently truncate (nextCursor null with reachable rows beyond the window) — the fixture mirrors this exactly (test-locked as recorded behavior) and it is registered for W2-12/backend planning.

**Role/capability behavior (the REAL registry):** `catalogue.read` = owner, org_manager, branch_manager, listings_editor; coach, front_desk, finance hold NO catalogue capability (Finance gains nothing from price options being money) — full seven-role matrix test-locked on both reads. Roles without `catalogue.read` never fetch listing data (queries disabled; spy-tested on index AND direct detail URL) and see a truthful "Listings are managed by your catalogue team" surface; **Listings is the portal's second capability-aware navigation item** (`requiredCapability: 'catalogue.read'`; the W2-6 nav lock test now pins exactly two capability-aware items). `listings.manage` is used ONLY to choose the empty state's informational copy — no mutation affordance exists anywhere (the empty state has no Create control; creation is honestly "an upcoming portal update", W2-8).

**Branch-scope semantics (PROVEN from shipped code, task §4):** the LIST filters to reachable listings — no active association at all (drafts not yet placed anywhere) OR ≥1 active association to an assigned ACTIVE branch (`some`-semantics; mutations use `every`, which W2-8 will mirror) — while the DETAIL read is organization-scoped ONLY: `getProviderProgram` applies NO branch-scope filter (scope constrains the list window and mutations, never the read — the same shape as W2-5's org-wide `org.read`). The portal mirrors both truthfully: a scoped Branch Manager's index lists exactly the reachable set (no counts/totals of unreachable rows anywhere), and a direct URL to an in-organization out-of-scope listing renders the read view with the scope explained ("runs only at branches outside your assigned branches… you can still read it here") plus "Assigned to you" marks on branch rows, exactly as the backend serves it. Unknown listing ids, foreign organizations' listing ids, and non-UUID segments (`/listings/new`) collapse into ONE byte-identical safe "Listing not found" surface (test-proven identical text; no title/state/org leak; no enumeration oracle).

**Lifecycle presentation (docs/24 §5.3 exact):** `draft` "Draft" · `submitted` "Submitted for review" · `in_review` "In review" · `approved` "**Approved — not published**" (copy states approval ≠ publication and who publishes) · `changes_requested` "Changes requested" (provider action needed) · `published` "Published" · `paused` "Paused" (explicitly reversible) · `archived` "Archived" (explicitly terminal). Chips are label-carrying (understandable without color). **Public visibility is never claimed from state alone:** the detail derives the COMPLETE canonical predicate from already-loaded truth — listing `published` ∧ organization `live` ∧ storefront `published` ∧ ≥1 active association to an active branch — showing "Customers can find this listing on Himma." only when all four hold, otherwise naming each unmet gate (all four gates unit-tested plus rendered examples: suspended Falcon's published listing shows "Not met — the organization is live on Himma").

**Price options (D-S4-1 exact):** ProgramPriceOptions render UNDER their one listing (a three-option program is ONE index row and one detail — test-locked); stable ids, deterministic `(sortHint, id)` order, `active`/`archived` state shown truthfully (archive-only retirement), integer-fils→AED display (`45_000` → "AED 450"; non-whole amounts keep two decimals), package `sessionsCount` shown, kind vocabulary exact (`dropIn/monthly/term/camp/package/free`). "From AED X" is a render-time convenience over ACTIVE options only — no `Program.price` exists anywhere (key-set-tested). **Offers stay conceptually separate** (own section, kind labels `Free trial/Paid trial/Discount/Promotion`, trial amount, effective window, active/ended state, plus explicit "separate from the pricing options above" copy) — never checkout math, never a price row.

**Branches/media/revision reads:** branch associations render the shared W2-5 branch truth (same labels/ids as the org view): active association+branch = "Active", deactivated branch = "Branch deactivated" (Sunset Open Water × Al Sufouh), removed association history = "No longer offered here"; foreign branches unrepresentable by construction. Media renders reference METADATA only (alt text, order, retired state) with an honest no-previews note — the Class-C media-binary/library gap is carried unchanged, no upload/cropper/URL resolution invented. `openRevision` renders READ-ONLY ("Changes pending Himma review." + submitted date + current-values-stay-live explanation; review-gated states without one show "No changes pending Himma review."); no revision creation/edit/cancel, no admin metadata. **Readiness (task §17):** drafts and changes-requested listings show a read-only mirror of the EXACT S4 completeness rules (English title · active taxonomy · ≥1 active branch association on an active branch · ≥1 active price option — the backend `CompletenessGap` vocabulary verbatim, all four unit-tested; Arabic optional, never required); the deactivated-taxonomy case is a real fixture (Synchro Performance Squad → inactive `synchronized-swimming` type).

**Index UX:** status filter + title search are honestly CLIENT-SIDE over the loaded rows (the real list API has no filter/search/status params — none invented, and no future contract is implied): filtering with further pages present shows "Filters apply to the listings loaded so far — load more below to include the rest.", empty-match is distinct from true emptiness, and the count line says "N listings loaded so far" while more pages exist. Empty catalogue: polished informational state (managers get future-oriented copy, no live CTA — W2-8 owns creation). Suspended organizations (W2-2 canon): reads stay with the canonical suspended banner on index and detail; no mutation affordances exist to disable.

**Adapter seam & fixtures:** `AuthRuntime`/`PortalPorts` gain `listingsPort` + `activityTypePort`. The SHARED fixture store ([fixture-auth.ts](portal/src/services/mock/fixture-auth.ts)) gained per-organization catalogue truth coherent with W2-2…W2-6 identities (same organizations, same W2-5 branch ids; onboarding `listingCount` now DERIVES from the same program rows). Scenarios: **Blue Wave** (12 listings across ALL EIGHT lifecycle states; flagship multi-option "Adult Beginner Swimming" with archived drop-in option, removed Sufouh association, media incl. retired ref, free-trial offer; pending-revision "Junior Swim Squad"; ladies-only listing; branchless draft; deactivated-taxonomy draft; no-price-option draft; archived listing with deactivated-branch association; ended discount offer) · **Noor** (2 listings) · **Falcon** (suspended, 1 published listing — visibility gates demonstrably blocked) · **Coral/stages orgs** (empty catalogues) · branch-scoped Salem (Marina-only reach) · listings_editor Nadia · no-catalogue-read seats (coach/front_desk/finance). New controls: `failNextListingsLoad` · `failNextListingDetailLoad` · `failNextActivityTypesLoad`. Fictional data only; no booking/session/payment rows exist to fixture.

Mirrored exactly (test-locked); fixing it is backend work, not W2-7's.

Activity labels are composed from the public taxonomy read (Class-A composition).
3. **Class B (observation):** no provider-facing read exposes moderation feedback for `changes_requested` (the admin decision stores no provider-visible reason on the provider contract) — the detail states action is needed without inventing a reason; registered for W2-9/W3 planning.
4. *(Carried, accepted/unresolved by owner instruction:)* MFA-enrollment/assurance read · invitation preview/resolution read · founding-owner composition · rejection-reason read · media binaries (Class C, restated by the media section) · opening-hours production wire encoding unratified (W2-12 must resolve before real writes) · provider-safe staff member display identity · production invitation email disabled — all unchanged.

## W2-6 · Provider Team & Staff UX (2026-08-08 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `4c83b72` (2026-08-08)**; owner reconciliation at closure (binding): the SHIPPED Owner-only `staff.read`/`staff.manage` registry is authoritative — the docs/29 §7 Team-row ◐ for Organization Manager was stale and docs/29 is reconciled to the implemented registry (matrix updated in place at W2-7; the backend capability registry was NOT changed); all previously accepted gaps and rulings carry forward)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-6):** the final Team experience at the approved routes `/o/:organizationId/team` (directory) · `/team/invite` (invite workflow) · `/team/:membershipId` (member detail, client-side composition), replacing the W2-1 placeholder. **Deliberately absent (later tasks/backends):** Listings/catalogue UX (W2-7+) · schedules/sessions/bookings/attendance/finance · verification evidence · internal admin functionality · customer-app changes · open self-registration (D-W2-2 stands) · any email infrastructure (fixture issuance models the existing backend command only) · real API calls · backend changes · W2-12 integration. Test-proven: no HR/payroll fields anywhere, no Team network calls, no token/digest in any rendered or persisted state.

**Staff domain truth (exactly the closed Slice-3 backend):** ONE composed read — `GET /provider/organizations/:orgId/staff` (policy `provider`, capability `staff.read`) returning ALL membership rows (`active` AND `revoked` history; fields `id/userId/role/branchScopeKind/branchIds/state/createdAt/version` — **no name, no email**) plus ALL invitation rows (`sent/accepted/revoked/expired`; email exposed here only). No member-detail or standalone invitation-list route exists — `/team/:membershipId` composes over the one read (recorded Class-A composition, the W2-5 pattern). Mutations mirror the three real `providerStepUp` routes verbatim: `POST .../staff/invitations` (supersedes any still-`sent` invitation for the same normalized address — the approved resend policy; response carries `invitationId/expiresAt/mailDelivery` and NEVER a token or acceptance link) · `POST .../staff/invitations/:invitationId/revoke` (idempotent on already-revoked; accepted/expired → `lifecycleConflict`; no version — a state CAS) · `POST .../staff/memberships/:membershipId/revoke` (REQUIRED `expectedVersion`; idempotent; `lastOwnerProtected`/`staleVersion` 409s). **No role/scope-change operation exists**: change is presented and executed as the canonical revoke + NEW invitation the person re-accepts with their verified email (docs/27 §5; memberships are append-only history — test-proven that the historical row keeps its original role identity, revoked). Invitation expiry is TIME truth: an overdue `sent` row renders as expired (acceptance refuses it pre-sweep) while revocation stays available; "re-send" is honestly a new issuance that supersedes.

**Role/capability behavior (the REAL registry):** `staff.read` and `staff.manage` are **OWNER-ONLY** (docs/24 §1.3; docs/27 §6 "nobody but owner touches staff") — tested for every one of the seven roles on the read and on each of the three mutations. **Recorded doc divergence:** docs/29 §7's Team row marks Organization Manager ◐ read-only, which the shipped registry contradicts; per docs/29 §2 ("UI authority derives ONLY from the backend capability model") the registry was followed — Organization Manager gets the same truthful no-access surface as the other five non-owner roles; the divergence is flagged for owner/W2-12 planning rather than silently resolved either way. Roles without `staff.read` never fetch staff data (query disabled; spy-tested) and see an honest "Team is managed by the Owner" surface — no fake disabled controls. **Team navigation is the portal's first capability-aware item** (`requiredCapability: 'staff.read'` on exactly one nav entry; PortalNav hides an item only when the backend-shaped capability list is KNOWN to lack it — usability only, the backend stays the boundary; no other item encodes a permission guess, test-locked).

**Seven-role vocabulary exact:** portal `PROVIDER_ROLE_LABELS` updated byte-identical to the backend canon (Owner · Organization Manager · Branch Manager · Listings Editor / Scheduler · Coach / Instructor · Front Desk / Booking Employee · Finance) — the pre-W2-6 shortened labels were a divergence and are gone; `ORG_WIDE_ONLY_ROLES` (`owner/org_manager/finance`) mirrored from `provider-roles.ts`; both test-locked against additions.

**Branch scopes:** the invite scope editor is role-driven — org-wide-only roles get a locked truthful "always covers the whole organization" (no meaningless checkboxes); the other four roles choose explicit **All branches** (a DISTINCT wire value, never collapsed into an id list) or a checklist of the addressed organization's ACTIVE branches only (deactivated Al Sufouh never offered; foreign branches unrepresentable by construction; empty selection refused client-side; the fixture re-applies the full backend validation — org-wide-only-role scope, empty, foreign, inactive, >50 all → `invalidBranchScope`). Scope display resolves branch labels from the shared org view, naming deactivated assignments truthfully.

**Owner safety:** the last-active-owner invariant surfaces two ways — the SOLE active owner's detail page replaces removal/change controls with a plain-language protection note (never a disabled-button fake), and any race (co-owner revoked concurrently) surfaces the backend's `lastOwnerProtected` refusal in provider words (no trigger/constraint vocabulary; test-asserted). Pending owner invitations never count toward the invariant (fixture mirrors the trigger's membership-only count). Self-management mirrors the backend exactly: an owner may revoke THEMSELVES when another active owner exists (the confirmation carries an explicit you-lose-access-now warning, and the portal re-resolves access instead of continuing stale — fixture emits `accessChanged`, the session machine reacts, and losing the only workspace lands on the canonical no-membership surface); the last owner cannot.

**Step-up choreography (first consumer of the W2-2 seam):** every staff mutation attempts first and reacts to the canonical `stepUpRequired` refusal (never a pre-emptive demand; reads never gated). The refused intent (full invite form values, or the targeted revocation) is preserved in a module-scope IN-MEMORY store ([team-pending-intent.ts](portal/src/pages/team/team-pending-intent.ts) — deliberately no browser storage, single-consumption take), the user routes through the existing `/step-up` interstitial with `returnTo`, and on return the originating surface rehydrates the intent for an EXPLICIT re-confirmation (info notice + the same dialog/form) — never an automatic retry, so one gesture can never execute twice (refused call executed nothing; spy-tested exactly 1 refused + 1 executed). The fixture models the real window semantics: fresh MFA sign-in or a completed step-up grant opens the 900-s window (`HIMMA_MFA_STEP_UP_TTL` default); `expireStepUpWindow()` forces the choreography in tests/e2e.

**CAS/concurrency:** membership revocation carries the loaded row's `expectedVersion`; a concurrent change yields the canonical conflict flow (focus-managed alert → "Load the latest team" → re-armed action succeeds with the fresh version; never silent overwrite, never raw version numbers). Invitation revocation is intentionally NOT versioned (idempotent state CAS — modelled as such, no imposed CAS). Duplicate-submit protection is layered: same-tick ref latches (kept latched through success/step-up handoff) plus phase guards plus busy buttons — the port-level tests additionally prove idempotent re-execution semantics.

**Identity & privacy:** members render from real fields only — role label, scope, joined date, state, and "You" (matched via the org view's own-membership id, the one identity seam the real contract provides). No email/name is invented or heuristically correlated for members; invitation emails render only where the real contract returns them (invitation rows). No userId/UUID, Cognito vocabulary, MFA state, token, digest, or internal id is rendered; read payloads are projection-locked (token stays internal fixture state; key-set tested).

**Adapter seam & fixtures:** [team/contract.ts](portal/src/team/contract.ts) defines `TeamPort` with EXACTLY the four real operations (test-locked; docblock records the deliberate absences: no member GET, no role edit, no resend op, no delete, and the known Class-B identity gap); unconfigured production default fails closed on every method ([unconfigured-adapter.ts](portal/src/auth/unconfigured-adapter.ts)). The shared fixture store gained per-organization staff truth coherent with W2-2 access seats via stable fictional `userId`s: invitation acceptance (W2-3 flow) now creates the actual membership row the Team read shows and the org view's `membership.id` is the caller's real staff row (the "You" seam); staff revocation removes the person's access seat (self-revocation emits `accessChanged`). Scenario coverage: Blue Wave (multi-role team, TWO active owners, branch-scoped manager, revoked-membership history, all four invitation states incl. overdue-sent) · Noor (exactly one active owner) · Falcon (suspended: read-only Team, pending invitation immutable) · Coral (draft org with NO owner membership until the founding invitation is accepted — invitations never count) · stages orgs (sole owners). New controls: `expireStepUpWindow` · `simulateConcurrentStaffChange` · `simulateConcurrentStaffRevocation` · `failNextStaffLoad/StaffMutation/InvitationMail`.

**Error vocabulary:** every rendered outcome maps a verified service/pipeline result — `invitationIssued` (incl. honest `mailDelivery: 'failed'`: invitation created, retry = new invitation) · `invitationRevoked` · `membershipRevoked` · `stepUpRequired` · `lastOwnerProtected` · `staleVersion` · `lifecycleConflict` · `invalidBranchScope` · `validationError` · `organizationSuspended` (reads still work; mutations refused with the shared canonical copy) · `forbidden` · not-found-shaped isolation (foreign/unknown org, membership, and invitation ids collapse into safe single shapes; the member not-found surface is byte-identical for foreign vs unknown ids — test-proven) · `unavailable` (retryable). Auth/session failures remain W2-2 surfaces.

**Contract gaps recorded (task §33):**
1. **Class B — provider-safe member display identity missing:** `GET .../staff` memberships expose `userId` only; no provider read returns a member's name or email, so the directory can label only the caller ("You") and render role/scope/joined truth for everyone else. A provider-safe display-identity field (and/or invitation↔membership provenance in the read) is registered for W2-12/backend planning. No heuristic correlation was built.
2. **Class B (operational, carried):** production invitation email delivery remains disabled (docs/26 D2; docs/27 §18) — `mailDelivery` honesty is preserved end-to-end; no email infrastructure was built.
3. **Doc divergence (needs owner/plan reconciliation, not code):** docs/29 §7 Team row (Org Manager ◐) vs the shipped owner-only `staff.read` registry — registry followed.
4. *(Carried, accepted/unresolved by owner instruction:)* the standing W2-2/W2-3/W2-4 gaps (MFA-enrollment/assurance read · invitation preview/resolution read · founding-owner composition · rejection-reason read · media binaries) **and the W2-5 opening-hours production-write ruling (encoding unratified; W2-12 must resolve the backend opening-hours contract before real writes)** — all unchanged.

**Verification (all green this commit):** portal tsc clean · ESLint `--max-warnings=0` clean · **Jest 352/352 across 33 suites** (30 port-semantics tests incl. exact-four-operations lock, seven-role vocabulary/label lock, full 7-role read+manage matrix, supersede policy, mail-failure honesty, scope validation matrix, explicit-all vs id-list, step-up gate with nothing-executed proof, idempotent revokes, last-owner incl. the concurrent-race path, self-revocation with access-coherence, canonical revoke+re-invite with history integrity, stale CAS, suspended read/mutate split, not-found shaping, acceptance→membership coherence, fail-closed port, no-network/no-Cognito greps · 14 directory tests incl. the six-role no-fetch matrix and nav capability awareness · 12 invite tests incl. duplicate-submit single-call and the full step-up round trip with preserved values · 12 member-detail tests incl. sole-owner, stale-reload-re-arm, change-flow hand-off, byte-identical not-found · 5 jest-axe suites over directory/no-access/invite-with-error/dialog/conflict — zero violations · all W2-1…W2-5 suites preserved; three W2-1/W2-3-era assertions updated for Team becoming real and capability-aware) · **Playwright 106 passed / 8 by-design skips** at 1440/1024/390 over the production fixture build (owner directory→invite→withdraw journey, member detail + step-up round-trip removal, stale conflict, read-only role incl. nav absence, last-owner protection, suspended org, org-switch isolation; zero console errors asserted per test; two W2-2-era specs updated for the capability-aware nav truth) · **default production build green and fail-closed**. Root/customer app untouched — no root regression triggered; no backend change (no PostgreSQL certification applies). **Screenshot evidence: `artifacts/portal-w2-6/`** (36 captures: populated directory with invitations, invite scope editor, invite success, withdraw confirmation, branch-manager member detail, remove-access confirmation, step-up interstitial + re-confirm, stale conflict, no-access role, last-owner protection, suspended — per viewport class).
