## W2-5 · Provider Branches UX (2026-08-08 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `fad8e5a` (2026-08-08)**; owner ruling at closure: the weekly opening-hours frontend representation stays for fixture/design purposes, but its production wire encoding is NOT ratified — W2-12 must resolve the backend opening-hours contract before real writes are enabled; this ruling is carried in the W2-6 section unchanged)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-5):** the final Branches experience at the approved routes `/o/:organizationId/branches` (index) · `/branches/new` (create) · `/branches/:branchId` (detail/editor), replacing the W2-1 placeholder. **Deliberately absent (later tasks/backends):** Team/staff management (W2-6) · listings and ProgramBranch association management (W2-7+, Listings workstream) · schedules/sessions/capacity/bookings/attendance/finance · map search · taxonomy administration · media upload · admin actions · real API calls · backend changes. Test-proven: no schedules/availability/capacity/staff/bookings/revenue data is displayed anywhere on the branch surfaces.

**Branch domain truth (exactly the closed S3/S4 backend):** reads come ONLY from the canonical org view (`GET /provider/organizations/:orgId` → `branches[]`, creation order) — the real backend has NO separate branch list/GET route, and `org.read` serves EVERY branch of the organization to every role; the W2-5 list/detail is a recorded Class-A composition over that read. Mutations mirror the three real routes verbatim: `POST .../branches` (`branch.create`) · `PATCH .../branches/:branchId` (`branch.edit`, CAS) · `POST .../branches/:branchId/deactivate` (`branch.deactivate`, CAS, idempotent). **Deactivation is one-way and is honestly presented as such** (neither the PATCH body nor any provider/admin route accepts `active: true`); it is never called deletion, history is preserved, and no reactivate control exists. Immutable ownership: `organizationId`, branch id, versions, and timestamps are never rendered as editable; no branch transfer, cloning, capacity, hours-truth beyond the real column, or virtual-branch concept was invented.

**Role/capability behavior (the REAL registry, not W2-4's `profile.edit` matrix):** owner + org_manager = create/edit/deactivate org-wide · branch_manager = `branch.edit` on explicitly ASSIGNED ACTIVE branches only (no create, no deactivate) · listings_editor, coach, front_desk, finance = read-only (`org.read` only on this surface). Read-only roles get truthful record rendering (definition list, explanation, zero disabled-form theater); unauthorized roles never see a fake "Create branch" CTA; the create route itself renders a truthful capability-denied surface. **Branch-scope UX (task §5):** a scoped Branch Manager sees ALL branches (mirroring the real org.read projection — scope limits mutation, never the read), with assigned branches marked "Assigned to you"; unassigned and deactivated-assigned branches render read-only with the scope explained; deactivation of an assigned branch removes edit reach (fixture mirrors the principal's assigned-ACTIVE-only resolution, empty scope never falls back to org-wide). Direct URL entry of an unknown branch id, another organization's branch id, or any nonexistent id renders ONE byte-identical safe "Branch not found" surface (test-proven identical text, no foreign branch data leak, no enumeration oracle).

**Create/edit (react-hook-form + zod, W2-4 pattern):** the zod schema mirrors the exact TypeBox limits (label 1–120 required · areaLabel 1–80 required · addressLine ≤240 · city ≤80 · geo ranges ±180/±90 pair-validated · facilities ≤20 × 1–40 chars); the fixture re-applies the same constraints server-side (`validationError` 422 seam). Editing builds a CHANGED-FIELDS-ONLY patch by comparing normalized values against the loaded record — untouched fields are never sent, so a save can never overwrite another writer's changes to fields this editor didn't touch. Every mutation carries the branch row's own `expectedVersion`; a stale writer gets the canonical conflict flow (focus-managed alert → "Load the latest branch" → base refreshed with this editor's edits kept dirty for review → save re-armed; never silent overwrite, never raw version numbers). Duplicate-submit protection proven (one port call). Create fixes the owning organization from route context (never an input), updates the SHARED fixture store, and navigates to the index. Deactivation: explicit action + ConfirmDialog with truthful generic consequences ("customers stop seeing this location; listings that run here can become unavailable at that location") — NO fabricated affected-listing counts (no endpoint provides them; test-asserted); an "only active branch" save-consequence note derives from real view data; stale deactivation refuses with reload path.

**Area behavior (task §8):** the branch contract carries `areaLabel` (string 1–80) — `branch.area_id` exists in schema but NO provider route reads/writes it (label→area reconciliation is registered backend/taxonomy-admin work, docs/28 §22). The picker is a read-only select sourced from the new `AreaReadPort.listAreas()` ⇄ public `GET /catalogue/areas` (active admin-owned rows, deterministic order; fixture shaped field-for-field: id/slug/labelEn/labelAr/city); selecting an area sends its label; free-text areas are impossible; no area vocabulary is hardcoded into components. A historical label no longer in the active taxonomy (fixture: "Al Sufouh") renders as the current, disabled, non-reselectable option and is never silently rewritten (dirty-comparison keeps it out of the patch; test-proven). Area-taxonomy load failure blocks the form honestly with retry.

**Opening hours (recorded assumption):** docs/24 §1.3/docs/27 §4 define `opening_hours` jsonb as "structured weekly hours (validated shape at the service layer; display formatting client-side)" — but the implemented service validates NOTHING (`Type.Any()` passthrough) and no JSON encoding is ratified anywhere. W2-5 implements the weekly editor over ONE conservative encoding kept in a single module ([opening-hours.ts](portal/src/branches/opening-hours.ts): `{mon…sun: {open:'HH:MM', close:'HH:MM'}}`, absent day = closed, null = not recorded), with TOLERANT reads: any unrecognised stored value renders "not set" and is only replaced if the provider deliberately edits hours (unit-tested). **Recorded gap/assumption:** this encoding must be ratified together with the promised backend shape validation before W2-12 writes it to production.

**Onboarding integration (task §13):** branch mutations invalidate the same shared caches (`['organizationView', orgId]` + `['onboarding', orgId]`) over the ONE fixture store — first active branch flips the hub's Branch requirement to Done; deactivating the only active branch flips it back; both proven, and branch completeness is test-proven not to move `verificationState` (completeness ≠ verification ≠ live). No separate `branchSetupComplete` flag exists. Suspended organizations (W2-2 canonical treatment): reads stay, every mutation affordance disappears (list, create route, editor, deactivation), and the port refuses `organizationSuspended` server-side regardless.

**Adapter seam ([branches/contract.ts](portal/src/branches/contract.ts) + [taxonomy/contract.ts](portal/src/taxonomy/contract.ts)):** `BranchPort` exposes EXACTLY `createBranch` / `updateBranch` / `deactivateBranch` (structurally asserted — no reactivate/delete/transfer/bulk/generic-CRUD member can exist), outcome vocabulary verbatim (`branchCreated/branchUpdated/branchDeactivated | staleVersion | validationError | organizationSuspended | forbidden | notFound | unavailable`), refusal ORDER mirroring the real pipeline (not-found shaping → capability → suspended-mutation → 422 → service branchNotFound → scope forbidden → CAS). Wired through `AuthRuntime`/`PortalPorts`; the unconfigured production runtime gains fail-closed branch + area ports (every operation `unavailable`; test-proven; default production build green and fail-closed). Fixture scenarios: Blue Wave owner (2 active + 1 deactivated historical-area branch) · org_manager (director@…) · NEW `manager@bluewave.demo` branch-scoped Branch Manager (Marina only) · NEW `frontdesk@`/`finance@bluewave.demo` read-only seats · coach empty-state · founding-owner Coral create journey · suspended Falcon · stale-conflict/transient-failure/area-outage controls (`simulateConcurrentBranchEdit`, `failNextBranchMutation`, `failNextAreaLoad`). Fictional data only.

**Recorded backend/contract gaps (task §28 — recorded, NOT worked around):**
1. **Class B — no provider reactivation contract:** deactivation is permanently one-way on the current surface (no provider or admin route can set `active` back); the UI says so plainly. A reactivation decision belongs to the backend owner.
2. **Class B — opening-hours shape validation absent:** the promised service-layer validated shape does not exist; the W2-5 encoding above is a recorded assumption pending ratification.
3. **Class A — no branch list/read route:** branch reads are composed from the org view (real contract; no invented endpoint).
4. *(Registered, not new:)* area label→id reconciliation (`area_id` dormant; provider contract is label-based until the reconciliation lands).
5. *(Carried, accepted:)* the five standing W2-2/W2-3/W2-4 gaps (MFA-read · invitation preview · founding-owner composition · rejection-reason read · media binaries) — unchanged by owner instruction.

Root/customer app untouched — no root regression triggered; no backend change (no PostgreSQL certification applies). **Screenshot evidence: `artifacts/portal-w2-5/`** (33 captures: list, editor, create, deactivated, scoped list + scoped read-only, read-only role, stale conflict, deactivation confirmation, empty state, suspended — per viewport class).

## W2-4 · Business & public storefront profile UX (2026-08-08 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `784c2e2` (2026-08-08)**; the recorded media-backend gap and carried W2-2/W2-3 gaps stay accepted by owner instruction)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-4):** the final Business Profile experience at the approved route `/o/:organizationId/profile` with the docs/29 §6 internal tabs — **Business information** (private record) · **Public storefront** (editor + customer preview) · **Publication** — replacing the W2-1 placeholder. **Deliberately absent (later tasks/backends):** branch CRUD (W2-5) · team management (W2-6) · listings (W2-7+) · media BINARY upload/library/cropper/signed URLs (no media backend exists; refs stay read-only) · verification evidence upload · admin actions · real API calls · backend changes. Boundary test-proven (no `input[type=file]`, no invite/branch/listing controls on the page).

**Binding domain split (S3 structural separation, docs/27 §3):** the UI is one experience but the model never blurs ownership. *Business information* renders the PRIVATE `organization` record read-only — `legalName` (only when the membership holds `org.legal.view`; absent for e.g. coach), `tradeName`, and the lifecycle state in provider language (`draft`→"Setting up" … `live`→"Live on Himma"), each private field carrying an explicit "Private / not shown to customers" treatment plus "contact your Himma representative to change" (no provider OR admin edit endpoint exists for these — truthfully non-editable). NOT rendered anywhere: `orgKind`, `origin`, `commercialTermsRef` (raw ref, no semantics), ids, versions, timestamps, audit data. *Public storefront* renders exactly the `organization_public_profile` mutable surface; *Publication* renders `published` + the visibility predicate. Private fields are test-proven never to enter the preview.

**Editor & role authority (canonical `profile.edit` = owner + org_manager, exactly the backend registry):** react-hook-form + zod (new approved deps: react-hook-form/@hookform/resolvers/zod, docs/29 §13) with the zod schema mirroring the REAL PATCH TypeBox limits (displayName 1–120 required-trimmed · descriptions ≤2000 · phone ≤32 · email shape ≤320 · website ≤300 · instagram ≤64); Arabic description stays optional and never blocks saving (English-only launch, §7). Editability = `membership.capabilities ∋ profile.edit` AND not suspended; every other role gets a truthful READ-ONLY rendering (definition list, no disabled-input theater) with "an owner or organization manager makes these changes"; suspended organizations render read-only with the canonical suspended copy regardless of role (mutations refused server-side mirrored by `organizationSuspended`). Form architecture: loaded defaults → dirty tracking (save inert + "All changes are saved" when pristine) → client zod validation (server `validationError` seam retained as backstop) → busy/duplicate-submit protection (one port call proven) → restrained `role=status` success → refetched base values merge via keep-dirty reset (background refetches never clobber in-progress edits).

**Optimistic concurrency (task §13):** every mutation carries the profile row's own `expectedVersion` (the real PATCH contract; org and profile versions are distinct). The PATCH body contains ONLY dirty fields, so an editor can never overwrite fields they didn't touch. A stale writer gets the canonical `staleVersion` → conflict alert (focus-managed): explains another edit won, offers **"Load the latest profile"** which awaits the refetch, keeps this editor's edits dirty over the new base for review, and re-arms the save with the fresh version — reload-and-reapply, never silent overwrite, never raw version numbers in copy. Save/publication success awaits cache refresh before announcing so a follow-up mutation can't carry a superseded version. Fixture control `simulateConcurrentProfileEdit(orgId)` (+`failNextProfileLoad/Save`) makes the scenarios real in dev/e2e.

**Storefront preview (task §8):** an explicitly-labelled customer-facing preview beside the editor (desktop two-column ≥1120px; stacked below), derived live from current form values and rendering ONLY the canonical `GET /providers/:organizationId` public projection — displayName, English description, the four public contact fields, ACTIVE branches (label · areaLabel · addressLine · facilities from real branch data), monogram fallback (the customer-app pattern; no media pipeline exists), and the derived Verified indicator ONLY when the organization is actually `live`. Honest per-state captions ("…once Himma takes your organization live and your storefront is published"). Test-proven absent: ratings, review counts, listing totals, opening hours, distance, booking affordances, private fields.

**Publication (task §9, canon-exact):** `published` is a field of the SAME `PATCH .../profile` under the SAME `profile.edit` capability (no separate endpoint; publish/unpublish audit split happens server-side) — represented as explicit Publish/Unpublish actions, never a state dropdown. The panel renders the full truth table: *Storefront published* ("You control this") · *Organization live* ("Himma controls this", link to setup status) · *Visible to customers* = live AND published, with published-but-not-live explained ("appears once Himma takes your organization live"). Unpublishing a LIVE storefront requires a confirmation dialog; non-live unpublish acts directly. No "Go live" control exists anywhere (provider authority doesn't include it); publication changes are test-proven to never move the lifecycle.

**Onboarding integration (task §11):** the W2-3 hub CTA already landed on this route; profile completeness flows back through the ONE shared fixture store — saving a display name flips the hub's Business-profile requirement to Done via query invalidation (`['onboarding', orgId]`), with no separate completion flag. After a save in a still-onboarding organization (draft/submitted/in_review/rejected/verified) the success message offers "Back to Getting started"; completion is test-proven not to imply verification or live. Saving a display name also re-resolves access so the shell/org-switcher identity updates (membership displayName derives from the profile, as in the real `/provider/me`).

**Adapter seam ([profile/contract.ts](portal/src/profile/contract.ts)):** `OrganizationProfilePort.loadOrganizationView` ⇄ `GET /provider/organizations/:organizationId` (field-for-field `OrganizationViewSchema` mirror: capability-shaped `legalName?`/`commercialTermsRef?`, full profile incl. media refs + `published` + `version`, branches, membership w/ capabilities) · `updateProfile(orgId, expectedVersion, patch)` ⇄ `PATCH .../profile` (outcome vocabulary verbatim: `profileUpdated{version} | staleVersion | validationError | organizationSuspended | forbidden | notFound | unavailable`; refusal ORDER mirrors the real policy pipeline: not-found shaping → capability `forbidden` → suspended-mutation refusal). Wired through `AuthRuntime`/`PortalPorts`; the unconfigured production runtime gains a fail-closed profile port (every operation `unavailable`; test-proven). Session/MFA failures stay delegated to the W2-2 machinery — nothing auth-shaped was reimplemented. Fixtures extended: full `organization_public_profile` state + own `version` per fixture org, richer branches (area labels, addresses, facilities), fictional legal names; scenario coverage = editable Owner (Blue Wave live+published) · editable OrgMgr (director@… on Blue Wave) · read-only coach (assistant@coral) · incomplete draft (Coral: empty display name, no branch) · unpublished (Sunrise) · **published-but-NOT-live (Pearl, verified+published)** · suspended (Falcon) · stale-conflict/validation/transient-failure via controls. No fake documents, metrics, or customer data.

**Media boundary (task §16):** the schema stores opaque media refs and the real PATCH accepts them, but NO provider media workflow exists (no upload/library/binary backend, no way to mint a ref) — the editor shows an honest "Logo & photos arrive in a later production milestone" section (reference-on-file copy when a ref exists), sends no media fields, and the preview uses the monogram fallback. Recorded below as the standing media dependency.

**W2-2 dev-fix disclosed:** the W2-2 session bootstrap effect used a once-only ref, so under React StrictMode's dev-only unmount/remount the single (cancelled) bootstrap attempt left dev builds stuck on "Preparing your workspace…" forever ([session-context.tsx](portal/src/auth/session-context.tsx) — now guarded by machine STATE so the remount re-runs the read-only bootstrap; production/Playwright behavior unchanged, all W2-2 machine/flow tests still green). Also additive: `InlineAlert` gained a `success` tone; new `TextAreaField` + `ConfirmDialog` UI primitives (drawer-pattern modal: focus trap, Escape, focus return, cancel-first).

**Unsaved-change protection (task §21):** router-level `useBlocker` (covers sidebar navigation AND the org switcher — org-switch-with-dirty-edits is specifically tested) renders an accessible "Discard unsaved changes?" dialog (Keep editing / Discard changes); `beforeunload` covers tab close/reload; clean navigation and tab switches within the page never warn.

**Recorded backend/contract gaps (task §28 — recorded, NOT worked around):**
1. **Class C — media binaries/library:** profile media references are PATCHable but no upload/storage/serving capability exists anywhere; provider-managed logos/photos need the media backend (already a registered later dependency). Until then the field set is honestly read-only.
2. *(Carried, accepted:)* the four standing W2-2/W2-3 gaps (no invitation preview read · founding-owner distinction by composition · no provider-facing rejection reason · no proactive MFA-enrollment read) — unchanged by owner instruction.
No new endpoint was invented; both consumed contracts exist verbatim in the closed S3 backend.

## W2-3 · Provider onboarding & verification-status UX (2026-08-07 — frontend-first over semantic fixtures; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `09e66a4` (2026-08-08)**; the recorded contract gaps below stay accepted by owner instruction)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §9/§16 W2-3 within the task's boundary):** the final invitation-acceptance experience at `/invitation/:token` (replacing the W2-2 inert placeholder) · the onboarding hub at `/o/:organizationId/onboarding` (docs/29 §6 naming) — status card, canonical checklist, submit/resubmit-for-review, honest verification-documents messaging · a shell "setup in progress" bar for non-live organizations linking to the hub (suppressed on the hub itself; never shown for live orgs; suspended keeps its own canonical banner) · one explicit readiness model · invitation/onboarding adapter seams for W2-12. **Deliberately absent (later tasks/backends):** business/profile forms (W2-4) · branch CRUD (W2-5) · staff directory/issuance/role management (W2-6 — acceptance only is W2-3) · listing CRUD (W2-7+) · any document upload or fake upload success · self-registration (D-W2-2 stands) · provider-triggered verify/go-live of any kind · W2-9 dashboard · real API calls · backend changes. Boundary-proven by tests (no form controls on the placeholder pages, no `input[type=file]` anywhere, no invite/upload buttons, no "create account").

**Invitation acceptance (canon-exact, docs/27 §9 + D-S3-1):** mirrors the real `POST /provider/invitations/accept` — body is the opaque token only; success returns `{ organizationId, membershipId }`; **every refusal is the ONE collapsed `invitationInvalid`** (unknown/expired/revoked/consumed/suspended-org/unverified-or-mismatched-email/already-member are canonically indistinguishable — the UX lists possibilities without claiming which, and fixture tests prove expired/revoked/unknown render byte-identical copy). Pre-acceptance the screen is GENERIC by necessity (no resolution read exists — recorded gap below). Auth is COMPOSED, not duplicated: signed-out visitors go through the W2-2 sign-in/MFA flow with `/invitation/:token` as a validated internal returnTo (the W2-2 access-zone returnTo block was relaxed for `/invitation` only); wrong identity → canonical refusal + "use a different account"; sessions expiring mid-flow fall back to the same state-driven page. Token hygiene: URL-only (canonical link mechanism), never stored (web-storage proven empty + the standing no-storage source scan), never echoed into copy/errors (DOM-scan tested), URL replaced on continuation. After acceptance the session re-resolves access (the machine now accepts `ACCESS_RESULT` from `noMembership`/`accessUnavailable` too — a real W2-2 gap this task exposed: a no-membership session could previously never GAIN access) and routes on the new membership: `organizationState === 'live'` → workspace dashboard (ordinary staff join, never forced through owner onboarding); otherwise → the onboarding hub. Founding-owner-vs-staff is this composition (role+state after `/provider/me` re-resolution) — the accept response itself carries no distinguishing field (recorded, not invented).

**Onboarding hub & readiness model ([derive-onboarding.ts](portal/src/onboarding/derive-onboarding.ts), pure, 13 unit tests):** stages map 1:1 from canonical `verification_state` (`draft→setup`, `submitted`, `in_review`, `rejected`, `verified`, `live`, `suspended`; unknown values render conservatively, never as live). Requirement items — Business profile · Branches · Team (optional) · Himma review · Go live · First listing (live only) — carry explicit states (`complete | actionRequired | awaitingHimma | blocked | optional`; the UI chips read Done/To do/With Himma/Waiting/Optional — never color-only). Completeness mirrors the EXACT backend submit rules (trimmed profile display name + ≥1 ACTIVE branch — the only two rules) and stays separate from verification, lifecycle, storefront publication, and listing state. **No fabricated percentage exists** (test-asserted). Actionability derives from the membership's REAL capability vocabulary (`profile.edit`, `branch.create`, `org.submit` — owner-only in canon, `staff.manage`, `listings.manage`): a coach sees full status with "An owner or organization manager completes this step." and no authority-implying CTA; step CTAs link to the honest W2-4/5/6/7 placeholder routes (no forms built). Submit/resubmit mirrors `POST .../submit` exactly (draft|rejected only; `expectedVersion` CAS): outcomes `organizationSubmitted | organizationIncomplete | lifecycleConflict | staleVersion | organizationSuspended | forbidden` all render safe provider-friendly copy, with stale/conflict auto-refreshing the snapshot; an incomplete org shows an inert (visually muted, explained) submit. Verification-documents area is HONEST backend-later messaging ("your Himma contact will arrange it… uploading documents is coming in a later production milestone") — no upload UI, no fake success, no invented upload API seam. Live orgs get the operational handoff (never trapped: no setup bar, dashboard link, first-listing prompt); suspended orgs get no go-live path and no submission.

**Adapter seams (no live calls):** [invitations/contract.ts](portal/src/invitations/contract.ts) (`InvitationPort.accept` on the real accept shape) · [onboarding/contract.ts](portal/src/onboarding/contract.ts) (`OnboardingPort.loadSnapshot` — explicitly documented as a COMPOSITION of `GET /provider/organizations/:orgId` + `GET .../listings`, field names verbatim; `submitForVerification` on the real submit route). The hub reads through TanStack Query (`['onboarding', orgId]`, invalidated after submission) — the first real domain query usage on the W2-1 foundation. Fixture implementations live inside the one fixture runtime (shared store with auth: acceptance mutates fixture memberships and emits the accessChanged interrupt; submission transitions fixture org state with version CAS). Unconfigured runtime: every port fails closed (`unavailable`/`providerUnavailable`); the production default remains the W2-2 unconfigured pair — proven again for the invitation route (renders the safe unavailable surface, no accept control) and at the port (acceptance without a session can never succeed).

**Fixture scenarios ([fixture-auth.ts](portal/src/services/mock/fixture-auth.ts)):** five new fictional orgs cover the canonical states — Coral Kids Climbing (fresh admin-created draft: empty profile shell, no branch), Sunrise Pottery Studio (submitted), Marina Chess Club (in_review), Desert Bloom Yoga (rejected → resubmittable), Pearl Divers Freediving (verified, go-live with Himma) — plus the existing live Blue Wave/Noor and suspended Falcon. New identities: `newowner@coral.demo` (founding-Owner invite target), `newcoach@bluewave.demo` (staff invite target into a live org), `stages@himma.demo` (owner across the four review states), `assistant@coral.demo` (coach — role-aware read-only). Demo invitation codes: `HIMMA-INVITE-OWNER-CORAL` · `HIMMA-INVITE-STAFF-BLUEWAVE` · expired/revoked demos (canonically indistinguishable refusals) · `HIMMA-INVITE-UNAVAILABLE` (transient failure). No fake documents or approved evidence exist anywhere.

**Recorded backend/contract gaps (task §28 classification — for owner/backend planning, NOT worked around):**
1. **Class B — no invitation resolution/preview read:** the only invitation surface is the accept POST, so a live UI can never show "you've been invited to X as Y" (or the target email) before acceptance; the pre-acceptance screen stays generic. If richer preview UX is wanted later, it needs a deliberate backend read designed against the §9 no-oracle posture.
2. **Class A — founding-owner vs staff distinction is composition-only:** the accept response carries organizationId/membershipId only; routing derives from the post-acceptance `/provider/me` membership (role + organizationState). Workable in W2-12; no flag invented.
3. **Class B — no provider-facing rejection reason:** admin verify/reject transitions record a `reasonCode`, but no provider read exposes it, so the rejected state can only say Himma will share what needs attention (docs/29 §9.7's "bounded reason" display is not currently satisfiable).
4. *(Carried from W2-2, unchanged by owner instruction:)* no proactive MFA-enrollment/assurance read for portal bootstrap.

**W3/admin + operational dependencies (visible, carried forward):** submitted/in_review/verified states cannot operationally progress at scale until the W3-minimum admin surfaces exist (start-review/verify/reject/go-live have backend routes but no internal frontend) **and** the VerificationCase/evidence backend exists — production `verified`/`live` transitions stay fail-closed (D-S3-3). Invitation ISSUANCE at scale additionally needs production email (D2). These remain scheduled dependencies, not W2-3 gaps.

MFA composition and token-free URL, wrong-identity refusal, submitted→rejected→resubmit→verified walk via org switcher, live-org no-trap; zero console errors asserted per test) · **production build green and fail-closed** · root untouched this task (no root regression triggered). **Screenshot evidence: `artifacts/portal-w2-3/`** (invitation, refusal, setup/submitted/rejected/verified hub states per viewport class).

## W2-2 · Provider access & session UX (2026-08-07 — frontend-first over the production adapter seams; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `cb8d038`**)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-2 within the task's tightened boundary):** the final access/session frontend — sign-in, login TOTP challenge, MFA-enrollment gate, step-up interstitial, signed-out/expired/no-membership/unavailable surfaces, one explicit session state machine, layered route guards, access-aware organization switching, account menu with sign-out, and the formalized adapter/access contracts for W2-12. **Deliberately absent:** real Cognito/SDK/network calls · real `/provider/me` · token handling of any kind · invitation ACCEPTANCE (structural `/invitation/:token` placeholder only — the workflow is W2-3 per the task boundary; docs/29 §16's broader wording noted) · onboarding/verification/profile/branch/team/listing work · backend changes. Everything is confined to `portal/` (root untouched this task).

**Access routes (docs/29 §6 naming):** `/sign-in` · `/mfa` (login TOTP challenge) · `/mfa/enroll` (mandatory-enrollment gate; the real QR/secret ceremony needs live Cognito and is honestly marked as arriving with it — fixtures never display TOTP secrets) · `/step-up` (re-auth interstitial: TOTP or single-use recovery code, mirroring `/auth/step-up/totp/*` + `/auth/step-up/recovery-code`) · `/signed-out` · `/invitation/:token` (inert placeholder; token never read/sent). Protected zone unchanged (`/o/:organizationId/...`) behind the guard layer.

**Session state machine ([session-machine.ts](portal/src/auth/session-machine.ts), pure reducer, 14 unit tests):** `bootstrapping → unavailable | signedOut(reason: initial|signedOut|sessionExpired, signInError?) → authenticating → mfaChallenge(submitting, error) → resolvingAccess → active(assurance, identity, memberships, stepUpExpiresAt) | noMembership | accessUnavailable`. Canon semantics preserved exactly: assurance is `'single_factor' | 'mfa'` (`ProviderAssurance` naming); revocation collapses into the one external `sessionExpired` outcome (the backend's deliberate `livenessOutcomeName` collapse — no invented `sessionRevoked` UX state); membership revocation is an access re-resolution to zero memberships; impossible transitions are structurally ignored; duplicate submits cannot double-fire (machine + `stateRef` guard + inert busy buttons).

**Auth adapter contract ([adapter.ts](portal/src/auth/adapter.ts)):** semantic operations only — `bootstrap` · `signIn` · `completeMfaChallenge`/`cancelMfaChallenge` (SOFTWARE_TOKEN_MFA login-challenge semantics) · `completeStepUpTotp`/`completeStepUpRecoveryCode` · `signOut` · `subscribe` (session interrupts). Outcome kinds reuse the backend vocabulary verbatim (`invalidCredentials`, `accountSuspended`, `sessionExpired`, `rateLimited`, `providerUnavailable`, `challengeInvalid`-derived `invalidCode`/`challengeExpired`). NO token material crosses the boundary in either direction; recovery codes are step-up-only (canon: they are NOT a login-challenge alternative — the sign-in MFA screen is TOTP-only by design).

**Provider-access contract seam ([provider-access/contract.ts](portal/src/provider-access/contract.ts)):** mirrors the real `GET /provider/me` field-for-field — `memberships[{ organizationId, displayName, role (7 provider roles), branchScope ('all'|uuid[]), organizationState }]`; active-only, offboarded excluded, suspended still listed. Capabilities deliberately NOT modeled here (the real contract exposes them per-organization via `GET /provider/organizations/:orgId`). **Recorded real-contract gap for W2-12:** no read endpoint exposes the caller's MFA-enrollment status/assurance (`GET /me` omits it; `mfaEnrolled` exists only server-side) — a live portal can learn it only reactively from `mfaRequired` outcomes; noted in-source for the integration milestone rather than inventing a contradictory contract.

**Guards ([require-provider-access.tsx](portal/src/app/require-provider-access.tsx)):** layered per docs/29 §6 — bootstrap/resolution renders a stable loading state (no protected flash, no fixture orgs while signed out) · signedOut → `/sign-in` with validated internal `returnTo` (deliberate sign-out → `/signed-out`) · `mfaChallenge` → `/mfa` · `single_factor` assurance → `/mfa/enroll` (MFA is never presented as optional; D-S3-5) · org id outside resolved access → safe workspace-unavailable surface (no info about the requested id, "go to your workspace" + sign out) · suspended orgs render with the canonical suspended banner (reads work; canon message verbatim). `returnTo` is open-redirect-proof ([return-to.ts](portal/src/auth/return-to.ts): internal-only, no `//`/backslash/encoded-slash smuggling, access-zone loops rejected). Frontend guards remain UX shaping only — backend stays the security boundary; `requiredCapability` seats stay null (no role guesses).

**Fixture architecture ([fixture-auth.ts](portal/src/services/mock/fixture-auth.ts), isolated in the mock boundary):** semantic scenario directory (shared demo password, TOTP `246810`, `000000` = lapsed challenge) — single-org Owner (Rana Haddad/Blue Wave) · multi-org director (Omar Farouk: Blue Wave + Noor + suspended Falcon Combat Academy) · unenrolled coach (`single_factor` → enrollment gate) · zero-membership `former@` · transient-access-failure `flaky@` (retry succeeds) · suspended account. Sessions are memory-only (a reload starts signed out — matching the future in-memory access token); dev/e2e controls (`window.__himmaPortalAccessFixture.expireSession()/revokeAccess()`) exist only in fixture mode. Structural safety proven by tests: no JWT/bearer/token strings anywhere in `src/`, no localStorage/sessionStorage/indexedDB, no stored password literal beyond the one documented demo constant.

**Production FAIL-CLOSED (task §25, mandatory):** [auth-mode.ts](portal/src/auth/auth-mode.ts) — dev defaults to fixture; a production build with no configuration resolves to `unconfigured` (adapter pair that can never authenticate; portal renders the safe "Sign-in isn't available yet" surface); fixture mode in a production build requires the explicit `VITE_PORTAL_AUTH_MODE=fixture` opt-in (what the Playwright run exercises); unknown values fail closed everywhere. `AppProviders`' default runtime is the unconfigured pair by construction; only `main.tsx` composes from the environment. Test-only `initialSessionState` seam is code-level (never configuration).

## W2-1 · Provider portal foundation & responsive shell (2026-08-07 — the real `portal/` application; **FULLY APPROVED and CLOSED at `11bab0d`**)

**Scope delivered (docs/29 §16 W2-1, exactly):** the production `portal/` package on the ratified D-W2-1 stack — application bootstrap, design-token baseline, responsive shell (desktop sidebar → tablet rail → mobile drawer), organization-scoped route foundation, org-switcher shell, honest placeholder pages, API/auth boundary seams, and the full QA harness. **Deliberately absent (later tasks):** any sign-in/session/Cognito behavior (W2-2) · any real API call (`/provider/me` included) · any domain workflow (onboarding, profile, branches, team, listings, lifecycle) · backend/customer-app changes.

**Package (`portal/`, npm-independent like `backend/`):** Vite 7 + React 19 + TypeScript 5.9 (strict, `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`, `verbatimModuleSyntax`) · react-router-dom 7 (data router) · @tanstack/react-query 5 · lucide-react icons · self-hosted Manrope via @fontsource (CSP-friendly, no font CDN) · ESLint 9 flat config (typescript-eslint + react-hooks + jsx-a11y) · Jest 30/@swc/jest on jsdom + Testing Library + jest-axe · Playwright (`e2e/`, runs the production build via `vite preview`). Root tooling excludes the package (root tsconfig/eslint), the root Jest pattern doesn't reach `portal/test`, and `.claude/launch.json` gained a `portal` dev-server entry. **Jest-environment note worth keeping:** React Router v7 needs TextEncoder/streams/fetch globals bridged into jsdom (`test/support/polyfills.ts` via undici); never bridge `MessageChannel` from `node:worker_threads` — React's scheduler builds a channel whose port holds the Node event loop open and Jest stops exiting (the bare `MessagePort` class is safe and is all undici needs).

**Design tokens (`portal/src/theme/`):** Orbit Indigo values from docs/07 mirrored as TypeScript source of truth + `tokens.css` custom properties (`--hp-*`) consumed by all component CSS; a Jest parity suite locks the two layers together and a structural scan proves no raw brand hex exists outside the theme layer. Desktop-density type scale on Manrope; 4-pt spacing; restrained radii/shadows; logical CSS properties (`inline`/`block`) used throughout for RTL readiness; `prefers-reduced-motion` globally respected; single-source shared-token package deliberately deferred per docs/29 §13 (recorded).

**Shell:** persistent 264-px sidebar (grouped icon+label nav: Workspace / Organization / bottom-pinned Settings & Support) at ≥1120 px → 84-px icon rail at 768–1119 px (CSS-only compaction) → hidden sidebar + top-bar trigger opening a modal drawer below 768 px. Drawer: `role="dialog"` `aria-modal`, focus moves in on open, Tab is trapped, Escape/close/backdrop dismiss, navigation closes it, focus returns to the trigger, and the background shell is `aria-hidden` + `inert` while open. Top bar: org switcher + inert, honestly-described Account placeholder (`aria-disabled`, no fake identity). Skip-to-content link (clip-until-focus pattern — a visible-shadow bleed from the translate-offscreen approach was caught in screenshot review and fixed). Active nav = soft indigo pill + inline-start bar + `aria-current="page"`. Backend-later sections (Schedule/Bookings/Finance) carry a quiet "Soon" tag (aria: "Coming soon"). Navigation metadata (`src/navigation/nav-items.ts`) carries an empty `requiredCapability` seat per item — presentation metadata for W2-2+, never authorization (test-asserted null today).

**Routes (docs/29 §6 naming, exactly):** `/` → first accessible organization's dashboard · `/o/:organizationId` + `/listings` `/schedule` `/bookings` `/branches` `/team` `/profile` `/finance` `/settings` `/support` · unknown org id → designed organization-missing surface · unknown section → in-shell not-found (nav intact) · unknown top-level route → standalone not-found · router `errorElement` + top-level React error boundary share one calm error surface. Access/auth routes are structurally absent until W2-2. Placeholder pages: correct titles (+ document.title pattern), one h1 each, short accurate descriptions, and two truthful timing lines — "Arriving in an upcoming portal update." (later W2 task) vs "Coming in a later production milestone." (Slice 6/7/W5 domains); backend-later pages are test-proven digit-free (no fabricated metrics/KPIs).

**Organization context/switcher shell:** org id lives in the URL (mirrors backend addressing); `AccessibleOrganizationsProvider` is the single seam W2-2 will feed from real `/provider/me` resolution — today it defaults to the isolated fixture (`src/services/mock/fixture-organizations.ts`: Blue Wave Swimming + Noor Learning Centre, the repo's fictional universe; UUID-shaped ids). The top-bar switcher is a `menu`/`menuitemradio` popover (arrow keys/Home/End/Escape, focus management, outside-click close); switching navigates to the same section under the new org; a single-membership set renders a plain context label (no button) — test-proven.

**API/auth/query seams (skeletons only):** `src/api/env.ts` (no hardcoded URL anywhere; `VITE_API_BASE_URL` per environment), `src/api/client.ts` (single client boundary; throws `ApiNotConfiguredError` unconfigured), `src/api/errors.ts` (the future 1:1 outcome-code→UI-state mapping location, documented), `src/auth/session.ts` (typed facade + binding W2-2 rules; no implementation, no storage), `src/app/query-client.ts` (per-instance QueryClient: staleTime 30 s, retry 1, no focus refetch; no domain queries).

## Backend Slice 4 · S4-6 closeout (2026-08-07 — final hardening audit and certification; **FULLY APPROVED at `a1e782d`** — **SLICE 4 CLOSED**)

**Audit outcome: ONE genuine defect found and fixed; everything else verified correct against canon.** The full docs/28 §18-style closeout matrix was re-driven across the finished system (schema `d98eb69`+`3ef57e7` · provider services `94a692c` · moderation `fecb8d0` · taxonomy admin `ceb3630` · public reads `c4fcfcf` · search `811defc` · this closeout commit). New suite [slice4-closeout.test.ts](backend/test/slice4-closeout.test.ts) (10 tests, RED-first for the defect).

**Fixed defect (the only one): trial-filter staleness across offer effective windows.** `has_trial` is refresh-time projection metadata, but an offer window opening/closing is pure time passage — no mutation, no event, no refresh — so a `trial=true` search could return a listing whose trial had ended (and miss one whose window had opened), permanently absent any other refresh. RED tests simulate the boundary crossing (direct window edit with no event), then the narrowest fix in [search-read-port.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/search-read-port.ts): the trial FILTER now evaluates a live `offer` EXISTS (state + kind + window at `now()`), the same live-truth discipline as the §6 visibility predicate. `has_trial` stays populated per docs/28 §9.11 as refresh-time metadata; offer badges were already live-hydrated (proven unaffected).

**Verified correct (evidence, no change needed):** responsibility coverage — every docs/28 §1 in-slice responsibility maps to an implemented module/route/suite (taxonomy schema+seed · Program · ProgramPriceOption · ProgramBranch · ProgramMedia · Offer · ProgramRevision · provider management + capability matrix · lifecycle · moderation · revision application · taxonomy admin · public detail/storefront-listings/taxonomy · projection + maintenance + SearchReadPort + `/search` · visibility · RLS record) · scope containment (structural table/DTO scans: no session/capacity/booking/payment/entitlement/rating/popularity object or field anywhere) · lifecycle machine (trigger-enforced edges, archived terminal, approval≠publication, no state-PATCH route, typed refusals without trigger/constraint names) · seven-role catalogue matrix incl. Cognito-claims inertness, immediate revocation, cross-org 404-shaping, MFA baseline, reserved-capability registration refusal (S4-2/S3-3 suites re-run green) · admin boundary (operations-only fresh per transaction; provider/customer/anonymous refused; no super-admin) · revision integrity (atomic application, frozen decisions, stale-drift refusal, no resurrection, `sensitive_fields_version` monotonic; NULL-means-unchanged stays a documented fail-safe limitation, unchanged by canon) · price-option invariants (stable ids, fils/AED, CHECK ties, archive-terminal, one listing per Program in every surface, derived-only fromPrice, no PriceQuote/entitlement behavior) · projection idempotency/ordering (duplicate/replay/stale-event/rebuild proofs re-run; refresh never mutates source rows) · **three-surface visibility parity** (detail = storefront collection = search across pause/republish with a deliberately stale ACTIVE document — the centralized `visibleProgramIds` predicate is the single authority) · DTO hygiene + privileged-bearer parity on all five public surfaces · ranking/filter audit (only canonical factors; unsupported dimensions still explicitly 422; collections resolve only supported presets) · **deep pagination** (per-sort complete walks under fully TIED score/price/recency keys with id tie-break; float score keys round-trip exactly; cross-sort cursor reuse refused; empty terminal page unreachable by construction — a cursor is minted only when an extra row was proven) · FTS/trigram plan evidence re-run · taxonomy integrity (11-category seed exact, slugs immutable, deactivation non-destructive) · branch/org isolation (composite-FK spine re-proven) · audit/outbox/inbox atomicity + payload hygiene · migrations 0007–0010 (checksums, migrate-from-zero, 0010 down/reapply, `db:verify`, zero codegen drift, no DELETE grants) · route inventory exact (Slice-4 surface: 19 `provider` catalogue routes · 18 `admin` moderation+taxonomy routes · 7 `public` reads incl. `/search`; `providerStepUp` deliberately unused by catalogue editing per docs/28 §16.2; no unclassified route can exist — registration aborts).

## Backend Slice 4 · search foundation (2026-08-07 — `program_search_document`, FTS/trigram, SearchReadPort, `GET /search`; **CLOSED: fully approved at `811defc`**)

**Scope delivered:** the docs/28 §9.11–12/§11–§13/§16.1 production search foundation — PostgreSQL is the launch engine (docs/23 §10.5 Tier 1; the §18.20 engine-tier decision stays open) behind the one `SearchReadPort` boundary. Search is LISTING-level across providers (Amendment A1): one Program = one result regardless of price-option count, every result carrying `provider { id, displayName }` back to its storefront. Backend-only; no frontend/session/booking/payment object; S4-6 closeout NOT started.

**Migration [0010_search.sql](backend/migrations/0010_search.sql):** `pg_trgm` (trusted core extension, 0003 pattern) + `program_search_document` — one row per listing: identity (program_id PK + composite org FK), `active` eligibility mirror, denormalized `title_en`/`display_name`, weighted English `search_vector`, taxonomy ids, `area_ids`/`branch_ids` (active associations to active branches; areas from `branch.area_id`), eligibility columns, `price_kinds[]` + `min_price_fils` over ACTIVE options (derived §14 metadata — still no `Program.price` anywhere), `has_trial` (current freeTrial/paidTrial offer), `published_at`, `rebuilt_at` watermark + standard conventions/immutable-identity trigger. **Indexes:** GIN(search_vector) · GIN trigram on title_en and display_name · btree(organization_id). Grants SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE — **no DELETE**: ineligible documents are DEACTIVATED, never deleted. Reviewed Down drops all 0010 objects incl. the extension (rollback + reapply verified). SCHEMA_CHECKS extended; codegen +1 interface, deterministic.

**Vector/ranking (docs/28 §11–§12, exact):** search_vector = setweight A: title · B: activity-type label + `synonyms_en` · C: category label · D: storefront display name (English config; Arabic additive later, never required). Match = FTS (`websearch_to_tsquery('english', q)` — no client search syntax reaches SQL) OR trigram similarity (`%`) on title/display name OR query-build synonym/label expansion against active activity types. **Deterministic score** = `ts_rank` + 2.0 exact-title boost + 1.0 synonym/label-matched-type boost + 0.5·greatest(similarity(title), similarity(display)) — ordered score DESC, `published_at` DESC, id ASC (published-recency tiebreak per canon; NO popularity/rating/personalization/recency-boost factor exists). Empty/whitespace query = filtered browse in published-recency order. Sorts: `recommended` (default) · `price` (derived value: free ⇒ 0, else min active option; no-option listings last) · `newest`; mock SortIds without authoritative data (`nearest/soonest/rating/popular`) are schema-refused.

**Filters (docs/28 §11 — real columns only):** `ladiesOnly` (gender = `women`) · `audience` adults/children (mock `suitsAdult`/`isChildRelevant` semantics verbatim) · `ageMin`/`ageMax` overlap · `areaId` · `categoryId` · `activityTypeId` · `formats` (price-kind overlap; camp = camp kind) · `setting` · `priceBand` (AED bands over the derived value) · `free` · `trial` (`has_trial`) · `skillLevel` (exact-or-`all-levels`) · `collectionId`. **Unsupported/future dimensions are explicitly 422-refused** (closed query contract + unknown-param guard): when/afterSchool/dayOfWeek/timeOfDay/nearMe/distance/offers/topRated/rating — reserved with their owning slices, never pretended. **Collection resolution is server-side** from the DB rows: `preset_ladies_only/child_relevant/camps/indoor` resolve; `preset_offers/available_today/after_school` stay inert until their authoritative data exists (0007-comment posture); unknown/unpublished collection ⇒ typed 404.

**SearchReadPort ([search-read-port.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/search-read-port.ts)):** `SearchReadPort.search(SearchQueryInput) → results|invalidCursor|collectionNotFound`; `PostgresSearchReadPort` is the only implementation; [search-routes.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/http/search-routes.ts) (`GET /search`, policy `public`, snapshot +2 lines) depends on the port only — no SQL in the route, no second search algorithm anywhere. Results reuse the approved public summary shape (`PublicListingSummary` + provider); hydration re-reads through `visibleListingQuery`, so display data (fromPrice/media/offer badges) is always live. Response-hygiene locked: no vector/rank/score/rebuilt_at/version/lifecycle/moderation/private field; privileged provider/admin bearers get byte-identical bodies.

**Visibility = public visibility (centralized):** [public-catalogue-read.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/public-catalogue-read.ts) now exports `visibleProgramIds(trx)` — THE single §6 predicate definition consumed by the listing detail, storefront listings, AND every search query (`psd.active AND psd.program_id IN visibleProgramIds`), plus again at hydration. Proven: a hand-poisoned ACTIVE document for a draft listing and an unprojected suspension both stay invisible — a stale document can never bypass public visibility.

**Projection maintenance ([search-projection.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/search-projection.ts), docs/28 §13):** every refresh recomputes ENTIRELY from live truth (event payloads are never applied), giving idempotence, duplicate/replay safety, and out-of-order safety structurally — proven by processing a stale `organization.suspended` event after reinstatement (document stays active). **(a) Same-transaction:** publish/pause/archive · non-sensitive hot-publish edits · branch association add/remove · offer add/update/end (`has_trial`) · sensitive-revision approval (moderation) · taxonomy label/synonym updates (activity-type/category refresh). **(b) Event-driven:** `processSearchProjectionEvents` consumes `organization.*` outbox events via the inbox-deduplicated `search-projection` consumer (no standing relay exists yet — callable consumer, Slice-1 relay deferral unchanged; the live predicate covers the window, proven). **(c) `rebuildAllSearchDocuments`** — idempotent full reconciliation; proven to never mutate source rows (program/org versions unchanged). Projection failure cannot corrupt catalogue truth (projection writes only its own table).

**Pagination:** opaque base64url cursor `{v:'psc1', s:<sort>, k:<full ordering key>}` — score+published_at(µs-precise text)+id for relevance, price-key+id for price, published_at+id for newest — so relevance pagination continues deterministically with no duplicate/lost rows (walk proven equal to the single-page order). Malformed/forged/sort-mismatched cursors ⇒ typed `invalidCursor`; limit 1–50 default 20; q ≤ 200 chars, whitespace-normalized.

**Query-plan evidence (in-suite):** with representative seeded documents, ANALYZE, and seqscan disabled, `search_vector @@ websearch_to_tsquery(...)` plans onto `ix_program_search_document_vector` and `title_en % …` onto `ix_program_search_document_title_trgm` (asserted in [catalogue-search.test.ts](backend/test/catalogue-search.test.ts)) — the canonical query shapes are index-served; no further tuning beyond the canonical launch design.

**RLS reconsideration (Slice-4 search was a recorded trigger): assumptions unchanged — deferral stands.** The projection is written by the same single pooled `himma_app` principal through application code; the event consumer adds ANOTHER cross-org background path that would need an RLS bypass (strengthening the docs/27 §18/docs/28 §20 analysis); no second DB principal, no direct-SQL/reporting access, and no portal GA arrived. Next triggers unchanged (second principal · direct SQL access · provider-portal production GA).

**Certification:** tsc clean · ESLint clean · **Jest 53 suites / 662 tests green** (new catalogue-search.test.ts: 23 tests written RED-first — schema/projection shape, one-doc-per-listing, visibility matrix incl. all eight listing states + org/storefront/branch gates, poisoned-document fail-safe, cross-provider `jiu-jitsu`/synonym/typo/exact-boost/tie-break ranking, full filter matrix + unsupported-filter refusals, collection resolution, service-wired maintenance, revision-approval price refresh, event-consumer idempotence/replay/stale-event proofs, DTO hygiene/parity, cursor walk + forged cursors, route inventory, listing/storefront identity regression, plan evidence; four prior boundary assertions amended for the legitimately-arrived surface) · migrate-from-zero (every suite DB, 10 migrations) · **0010 rollback verified** (down removes table + pg_trgm; reapply + verify green) · `db:verify` green (checksums/immutability; 0010 SCHEMA_CHECKS) · codegen zero drift · route-policy snapshot intentionally +2 (`GET|HEAD /search → public`) · no frontend change (customer app stays mock-driven; no native recertification).

**Remaining Slice-4 responsibility (unstarted; owner approval required):** S4-6 hardening/closeout. Customer frontend integration stays a separately approved milestone.

## Backend Slice 4 · public catalogue reads (2026-08-07 — customer-public listing detail, provider storefront listings, public taxonomy reads; **CLOSED: fully approved at `c4fcfcf`**)

**Scope delivered:** the docs/28 §16.1/§19 customer-public read surface over the closed S4-1 schema — PUBLIC READ ONLY: no mutation, no search route/index/projection, no session/capacity/booking/payment object, no frontend change (the customer app stays mock-driven until its integration milestone; no native recertification needed). New [public-catalogue-read.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/public-catalogue-read.ts) + [public-catalogue-routes.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/http/public-catalogue-routes.ts); registered unconditionally beside the S3-4 storefront read (public surface, no MFA/production-gate dependency). No migration; migrations 0007–0009 untouched.

**Six routes (all explicitly `public`; anonymous; a privileged provider/admin bearer receives the byte-identical projection — proven; snapshot intentionally +12 lines incl. HEAD):** `GET /listings/:programId` (public listing detail) · `GET /providers/:organizationId/listings` (paginated published listings of one storefront — the §19 dedicated endpoint; the approved S3-4 `GET /providers/:organizationId` payload is untouched and regression-locked) · `GET /catalogue/categories` · `GET /catalogue/activity-types` · `GET /catalogue/collections` · `GET /catalogue/areas`. New typed outcome: `invalidCursor` (422).

**Visibility (docs/28 §6, evaluated live per request — no denormalized projection exists):** `program.listing_state='published'` AND `organization.verification_state='live'` AND `organization_public_profile.published=true` AND ≥ 1 ACTIVE `program_branch` association to an ACTIVE branch. Everything else — draft/submitted/in_review/approved-but-unpublished (D-S4-2 proven: approval alone never exposes)/changes_requested/paused/archived listings, verified-not-live/suspended/offboarded orgs, unpublished storefronts, nonexistent ids — is one byte-identical 404 (no lifecycle oracle). Suspension, listing pause/archive, storefront unpublish, association removal, and branch deactivation all bite on the next request; reinstatement/reactivation restores (all proven).

**Projections (explicit mappings, never serialized rows):** closed `PUBLIC_LISTING_SOURCES` column lock with the S3-4 structural discipline (lock lists asserted against the live schema; a scratch private column with a sentinel provably cannot leak). Detail = id/titles/descriptions/setting + §2.5 eligibility + `category`/`activityType` refs (id/slug/labelEn/labelAr) + `provider { id, displayName }` (the storefront link is one hop) + active public branches (the S3-4 branch shape) + active ordered media references (`mediaRef`+alt only) + active ProgramPriceOption summaries + currently-applicable informational Offers + derived `fromPrice`. Storefront rows are one summary per Program (never per option) with taxonomy refs, eligibility, media, `fromPrice`, and offer-kind badges. NO `listing_state`/version/timestamps/moderation/revision/audit/staff/legal/commercial field anywhere; no snake_case; no fake marketplace data (rating/schedule/availability/capacity/booking fields structurally absent — scanned).

**Price (D-S4-1/§14):** no `Program.price` exists (schema-asserted). `fromPrice` derives at read time: `{kind:'free'}` when an active `free` option exists, else `{kind:'from', amountFils:min, currency:'AED'}` over ACTIVE options, `null` when none — recomputed immediately when the cheapest option archives (proven). Option summaries carry stable ids (+ kind/amountFils/currency/sessionsCount/labels; `sessionsCount` is §9.6b catalogue description needed for kind-derived package display — recorded realization note). Offers stay a distinct structure (`freeTrial` etc. never appear as price options); "currently applicable" = `state='active'` AND inside the effective window at request time.

**Public taxonomy (D-S4-3):** categories active-only in (sort_hint, slug) order — the docs/15 §3 eleven seeded categories lead in seed order; activity types active AND parent-category-active (recorded note: the smallest rule keeping the public two-level relationship from dangling onto an invisible category), ordered (category sort, slug), synonym arrays never serialized (search-internal); collections `published`-state editorial fields only — the preset booleans are deliberately NOT public (collection→results resolution is the search commit's server-side concern per §11); areas active-only in (sort_hint, slug) order. Arabic NULL never suppresses any row; no version/CAS/sort_hint metadata serializes.

**Pagination (docs/24 §11.4):** storefront listings limit 1–50 (default 20), stable creation (UUIDv7 `program.id`) order with keyset continuation — cursor is opaque base64url (`plc1:<uuid>` payload), stable across pages, never a raw id; malformed/wrong-payload cursors → typed `invalidCursor`; walk proven complete with no duplicate/missing rows; a visible storefront with zero eligible listings is an EMPTY page (200), an ineligible/unknown storefront is the byte-identical 404.

**Query/index review:** detail rides the `program` PK; the storefront page rides `ix_program_org_state (organization_id, listing_state)` + the `program_branch`/`branch` PKs for the EXISTS probe; taxonomy tables are small admin-owned data. No non-search index is demonstrably needed at launch scale → no migration (per the task's smallest-justified rule). **RLS reconsideration: assumptions unchanged** — these are public reads over explicitly projected data (exactly the docs/28 §20 "(b)" case); the deferral and its triggers stand untouched.

**Certification:** tsc clean · ESLint clean · **Jest 52 suites / 639 tests green** (new [catalogue-public.test.ts](backend/test/catalogue-public.test.ts): 20 tests written RED-first covering the full task matrix; the two prior boundary assertions in the moderation/taxonomy-admin suites amended for the legitimately-arrived public surface) · migrate-from-zero (every suite database) · `db:verify` green (checksums + schema checks; migration immutability held) · codegen zero drift · route-policy inventory exact-matched · no migration added · working tree confined to `backend/` + docs.

**Remaining Slice-4 responsibilities (unstarted; owner approval required):** search foundation (`program_search_document`, FTS/trigram, `SearchReadPort`, §11–§13) · S4-6 hardening/closeout.

## Backend Slice 4 · taxonomy administration (2026-08-07 — internal `/admin/taxonomy` management; **CLOSED: fully approved at `ceb3630`**)

**Scope delivered:** production internal-admin administration of the four S4-1 taxonomy entities (`area`, `category`, `activity_type`, `collection`) per docs/28 §16.3 — DATA management only; the database stays authoritative, no schema change was needed (migrations 0007–0009 untouched; `db:verify` checksums green). New [taxonomy-admin.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/taxonomy-admin.ts) + [admin-taxonomy-routes.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/http/admin-taxonomy-routes.ts).

**Nine routes (all `admin` policy + fresh per-transaction `operations` role — same shared check as moderation; snapshot intentionally +10 lines incl. HEAD):** `GET /admin/taxonomy` (full administration view incl. inactive rows) · `POST|PATCH /admin/taxonomy/areas[/:areaId]` · `…/categories[/:categoryId]` · `…/activity-types[/:activityTypeId]` · `…/collections[/:collectionId]`. Support/finance/auditor/access-admin, provider bearers, customer bearers, Cognito claims, and anonymous callers are all refused; revocation bites immediately.

**Semantics (canon-exact, nothing invented):** slugs immutable (structurally inexpressible in PATCH bodies + the 0007 trigger; unique violations surface as typed `slugConflict`) · English labels required, Arabic always optional (D-S4-3) · deterministic ordering via `sort_hint` · **deactivation, never deletion** — proven reference safety: deactivating an in-use activity type/category/area leaves `program.activity_type_id`, `branch.area_id`, program state, and branch rows byte-untouched (no cascade, no rewrite, no unpublish), while S4-2 provider validation immediately refuses the deactivated type for NEW listings; reactivation is a plain `active=true` (canon's active/inactive flag is not a terminal machine) · activity-type creation requires a real parent category (typed `invalidTaxonomy`); **re-parenting is deliberately not an operation** (canon defines none) · collection editorial fields incl. the typed preset booleans and the `draft|published|archived` state value set (the 0007 CHECK; canon defines no transition edges, so none were invented) · CAS `expectedVersion` on every PATCH; refused mutations emit nothing. Audit + outbox in the same transaction: `taxonomy.area_changed|category_changed|activity_type_changed|collection_changed` (aggregate `taxonomy`; payload = entity id + bounded `created|updated` slug). Seed integrity proven: the 11 seeded categories + 2 entry-12 lens collections remain exact and duplicate-free under administration.

**Recorded, not invented:** the future search projection (docs/28 §13) will consume the `taxonomy.*_changed` outbox events for document refresh — no search infrastructure exists yet · area launch content remains admin-created data (no approved seed list; the S4-1 note stands) · **RLS reconsideration: assumptions unchanged** — taxonomy is admin-owned data with no per-organization isolation dimension (exactly the docs/28 §20 analysis), so the documented deferral and its triggers stand untouched.

## Backend Slice 4 · moderation (2026-08-07 — internal catalogue moderation + ProgramRevision decisions; **CLOSED: fully approved at `fecb8d0`**)

**Scope delivered:** the internal Himma review path `provider submit → review → approved | changes_requested → provider publication` and the admin decision path for provider-submitted `program_revision` records, on the closed S4-1 schema and S4-2 services. New [moderation.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/moderation.ts) + [admin-moderation-routes.ts](backend/src/modules/catalogue/http/admin-moderation-routes.ts); no new tables, states, capabilities, or role semantics — authority is the EXISTING `admin` policy (B2-6C: live session + MFA assurance + recent factor + ≥1 active DB admin role) with the `operations` role re-checked fresh inside every transaction (docs/24 §10.2; docs/28 §6/§16.3). Support/Finance/Access-Admin/Auditor, provider bearers, and customer bearers are all refused; revoked admin authority bites on the next request; Cognito claims stay inert.

**Nine named-action routes (docs/28 §16.3; all `admin` policy; route-policy snapshot intentionally +12 lines incl. HEAD):** `GET /admin/listings` (review queue by state, paginated) · `GET /admin/listings/:programId` (moderation projection: full ProgramDetailView + organization identity/state + FULL structured open-revision change set; explicit TypeBox contract shared with the provider surface) · `POST …/review/start|approve|request-changes` (submitted→in_review→approved|changes_requested; bounded `reasonCode` slug only, never free-text notes) · `GET /admin/revisions` (revision queue) · `POST …/revisions/:revisionId/review/start|approve|reject`. **D-S4-2 holds: approval RESTS at `approved`** — publication remains the provider `listings.publish` action; no writable state field exists anywhere.

**Revision decisions (docs/28 §7):** machine `submitted → in_review → approved | rejected`, decided rows frozen. Approval applies the structured change set ATOMICALLY in the decision transaction: eligibility/safety-copy fields onto the program, the option change onto its stable target (or a NEW option id for an add-intent), `sensitive_fields_version` bumped exactly once — with `listing_state` untouched (published stays published; approved stays approved). All typed-refusal prechecks run BEFORE any mutation (merged-eligibility and merged-option-shape validation, archived-listing and archived-target-option conflicts), so a refusal can never commit a partial application; the decision CAS is the last fallible step. Stale drift (e.g. an out-of-band-archived target option) refuses with `lifecycleConflict`, leaves the revision in_review for rejection, and archived options can never be edited or resurrected. Double decisions fail with typed conflicts (one event, one `sensitive_fields_version` bump — proven). Events (same transaction, ids/safe slugs only): `listing.review_started/approved/changes_requested` · `listing.revision_approved/rejected` · reconciliation note: `listing.revision_review_started` added as the smallest consistent namespace entry for the review-start state change docs/28 §15 names no event for. Second reconciliation note (schema semantics): a NULL revision change column means "unchanged" — clearing a sensitive field to NULL is not representable in the approved §9.10 structure (a future owner/schema decision if ever needed).

**Certification:** tsc clean · ESLint clean · **Jest 50 suites / 605 tests green** (new [catalogue-moderation.test.ts](backend/test/catalogue-moderation.test.ts): 18 tests covering the full §18-style matrix — role matrix, surface separation, review machine walks, approval-never-publishes, CAS races, double-decision, stale-option refusal, ownership immutability, payload hygiene, exact route inventory; two S4-2 boundary assertions amended because moderation legitimately arrived) · migrate-from-zero · `db:verify` · codegen zero drift (no schema change).

**Remaining Slice-4 responsibilities (unstarted; owner approval required):** internal taxonomy administration (§16.3 taxonomy CRUD) · customer-public listing detail + storefront-listings + catalogue reads (§16.1/§19) · search foundation (`program_search_document`, FTS/trigram, `SearchReadPort`, §11–§13) · S4-6 closeout.

## Backend Slice 4 · S4-2 (2026-08-07 — provider catalogue services, capability activation, and §16.2 provider-private routes; **CLOSED: fully approved at `94a692c`**)

**Capability activation (docs/28 §1.5/§6; D-S4-2):** the S3-3 registry gains four ACTIVE capabilities — `catalogue.read` (owner, org_manager, branch_manager, listings_editor) · `listings.manage` (same four; branch_manager service-enforced within branch scope; also carries offer + branch-association + price-option management — `offers.manage` deliberately stays RESERVED because docs/28 §1.5 activates exactly these four) · `listings.publish` (**Owner + Organization Manager ONLY**) · `media.manage` (the listings.manage set). Coach, Front Desk, and Finance gain nothing (Finance acquires no access from monetary catalogue metadata). Exact-matrix test: [catalogue-capabilities.test.ts](backend/test/catalogue-capabilities.test.ts).

**New `catalogue` module** ([services](backend/src/modules/catalogue/services/catalogue-shared.ts) + [routes](backend/src/modules/catalogue/http/catalogue-routes.ts)): program create/list/detail/edit + named lifecycle actions (submit/publish/pause/archive — publication is NEVER a state patch; PATCH bodies are additionalProperties-stripped so a smuggled `listingState` is inexpressible), price-option add/edit/reorder/archive (stable ids, archive-only, typed `invalidPriceOption` prechecks over the S4-1 CHECK ties), branch associations (active same-org branches only; reactivating a historical row preserves history), media references (metadata only), offers (structured; Offer ≠ ProgramPriceOption). **Sensitive-edit routing (docs/28 §7):** on approved/published/paused listings, eligibility/safety-copy patches and EVERY price-option change create a `program_revision` (submitted, one open per listing → `revisionPending`) without touching live fields; mixed patches apply non-sensitive fields and defer sensitive ones; submitted/in_review listings refuse provider edits; drafts edit directly. **Completeness** is structured (`programIncomplete` + `missing: title|activeTaxonomy|activeBranch|activePriceOption`) at submit AND publish; publish additionally requires org `live` (`organizationNotLive`). Branch-scoped staff mutate a listing only while every active association is in their scope, associate only their own branches, and list only reachable listings. All 19 routes declare `provider` policy + capability (MFA baseline; no step-up — catalogue editing is not in the D-S3-5 higher-risk set per docs/28 §16.2); the production surface shares the provider fail-closed MFA gate in [build-app.ts](backend/src/app/build-app.ts). Audit/outbox (same transaction, ids-only payloads): `listing.created/updated/submitted/published/paused/archived/revision_submitted/branch_association_changed/media_changed/price_option_changed/price_option_archived` + `offer.created/updated/ended`. New HTTP vocabulary: `organizationNotLive`, `revisionPending`, `invalidTaxonomy`, `invalidEligibility`, `invalidPriceOption`, `invalidOffer`, `programIncomplete` (with `missing`).

Because 0008 is checksum-immutable, the fix is the corrective migration [0009_gender_eligibility_correction.sql](backend/migrations/0009_gender_eligibility_correction.sql): recodes any dev/test `ladies` rows to `women` (user triggers suspended for the mechanical recode only — a privilege `himma_app` provably lacks), then replaces the two CHECKs (`program.gender_eligibility`, `program_revision.gender_eligibility`); reviewed lossy Down (dev/test only: `women`→`ladies`, `girls|boys`→`mixed`). Real-PostgreSQL proofs added in program-schema.test.ts: all five values succeed on program AND revision; legacy `ladies` and arbitrary values rejected; girls/boys never age-inferred or rewritten on age edits; English-only creation, lifecycle, option, and cross-org suites unchanged. ## Backend Slice 4 · specification (2026-08-07 — `docs/28` approved in structure at `6b60fb2`; Amendment A1 applied at `b016c85`, **fully approved and binding**)

### docs/28 Amendment A1 (2026-08-07 — the owner's D-S4-1…3 rulings, applied in place; docs/24 Amendment A2 in the same commit)

- **D-S4-1 DECIDED — multi-option listings:** one canonical Program may carry multiple commercial **ProgramPriceOption** children (Program = discoverable concept; option = commercial way to purchase it: `Adult Beginner Jiu-Jitsu` trial/monthly/3-months · `Gym Access` monthly/3-month/annual · `Swedish Massage` 60/90-min/5-session package). Binding modelling rule: options never collapse materially different experiences (adult vs children, private vs group, different activities/content/search intent = separate listings). Options are catalogue/commercial metadata ONLY — never capacity/booking/entitlement/payment/attendance/redemption records; PriceQuote/checkout untouched. No authoritative single `Program.price`; the exact derived-field boundary (`From AED X` = min over active options; single-option direct display; id/kind/amount/label summaries for future option selection BY stable ID) is docs/28 §14. Option changes on published listings are sensitive-field revisions. **docs/24 Amendment A2** rewrites §2.2 (ProgramPriceOption entity row), §2.6, and §12.1 so no single-price wording survives in canon; the mock's single price maps to one option per listing.
- **D-S4-2 DECIDED — publication:** auto-publish-on-approval default OFF; approval ≠ publication; publish/unpublish = Owner + Organization Manager only; Listings Editor submits but never publishes; Branch Manager/Finance/Coach/Front Desk hold zero publication authority; no moderation bypass; parent-org visibility stays authoritative.
- **D-S4-3 DECIDED — taxonomy seed:** docs/15 §3 twelve entries as database-managed seed content (stable ids/slugs, deterministic ordering, active/inactive, English launch, localization-ready; Arabic never blocks); taxonomy changes are migration/admin-data, never frontend constants; no commercial behavior from taxonomy names.
- **Clarification (binding):** search results are one per LISTING across providers (`jiu-jitsu` → listings from many organizations, never one per membership duration), each retaining `Listing → Organization → Public Provider Storefront`.

**`docs/28_BACKEND_SLICE_4_CATALOGUE_LISTINGS_AND_SEARCH_PLAN.md`** — the owner-review specification for Catalogue, Listings/Offerings, Classification, and Search Foundation, per the docs/26/27 planning pattern. Documentation only; approval starts nothing by itself. Key shapes: the binding marketplace model (search at the listing level across providers; `Organization → Storefront → Branch → Listing (Program)` spine attached via `program.organization_id` + same-org `program_branch` composite FKs — the docs/27 §12.8-proven shape) · one catalogue model for gyms/academies/spas/kids-providers/camps through `activity_type` + price kinds + offers, never per-vertical schemas · the docs/24 §5.3 listing machine applied verbatim with a structured `program_revision` sensitive-field flow (published version stays live during review; no approval bypass exists structurally, so no extra capability gate is needed — analyzed in §8) · taxonomy/area/collection as admin-owned seeded DATA (docs/15 §3 twelve entries; slugs immutable; the S3-1 `branch.area_id` FK finally lands) · eligibility levels kept distinct (listing ≠ option ≠ session ≠ booking-time revalidation) · compound public visibility (`published` listing AND live+published org AND ≥1 active branch) applied as a query-time predicate so search/projections can never expose a suspended provider even with a stale document · **search recommendation: staged, PostgreSQL-native at launch** (FTS + pg_trgm + taxonomy synonyms over a rebuildable `program_search_document`, behind a `SearchReadPort`; docs/23 §18.20 engine-tier decision left open) · storefront-listings served by a dedicated paginated public endpoint (S3-4 projection untouched) · capability ACTIVATION (`listings.manage/publish`, `media.manage`, `catalogue.read`) on the existing S3-3 registry · **RLS: continue deferring** (re-evaluated per the S3-5 trigger; §20 records the next triggers) · proposed migrations 0007 (taxonomy) + 0008 (catalogue) with full DDL-review detail · six-commit plan S4-1…S4-6, each stop-for-approval · mock→production mapping confirmed against the approved docs/24 §12 (no customer-page redesign; schedule-derived fields explicitly pending Slice 6; frontend NOT connected in Slice 4).

**§22 owner decisions — BLOCKING Slice-4 schema:** **D-S4-1** listing↔priced-option multiplicity (recommend: additive `program_price_option` child ruled now; canon-as-is deferral is the safe fallback — the schema seam keeps either additive) · **D-S4-2** publication act (recommend: auto-publish-on-approval default OFF; `listings.publish` = owner + org_manager) · **D-S4-3** ratify the docs/15 §3 taxonomy seed.
